=== ASSETS.MD ===
# Duralux asset manifest — what every page must include, and in what order

Paths are relative to the site root (pages live next to `assets/`).

## Required on EVERY page (app and auth), exact order

Head CSS (in `<head>`, in this order):

```html
<link rel="shortcut icon" type="image/png" href="assets/images/favicon.png">
<link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="assets/css/bootstrap.min.css">   <!-- 1. Bootstrap 5.3 (theme build) -->
<link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="assets/vendors/css/vendors.min.css"> <!-- 2. vendor bundle -->
<!-- 3. page-specific vendor CSS here (see per-page table) -->
<link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="assets/css/theme.min.css">       <!-- 4. ALWAYS LAST: theme overrides -->
```

Bottom JS (end of `<body>`, in this order):

```html
<script src="assets/vendors/js/vendors.min.js"></script>  <!-- 1. ALWAYS FIRST ("always must need to be top"): jQuery + Bootstrap + PerfectScrollbar + pace + nxlNavigation + full-screen-helper etc. -->
<!-- 2. page-specific vendor JS here (see per-page table) -->
<script src="assets/js/common-init.min.js"></script>          <!-- 3. shared shell init (required on every page) -->
<!-- 4. page-specific init here, e.g. customers-init.min.js -->
<script src="assets/js/theme-customizer-init.min.js"></script> <!-- 5. ALWAYS LAST: skins/dark-mode (also powers the header dark/light toggle) -->
```

Ordering rules that matter:
- `theme.min.css` must come after all vendor CSS or component skins break.
- `vendors.min.js` must load before anything else (jQuery lives there).
- `common-init.min.js` must come before the page init and after all vendor JS.
- `theme-customizer-init.min.js` is included on every page, even pages without the customizer
  panel, because it applies the persisted skin/font from localStorage on load and drives the
  header `.dark-button`/`.light-button` toggle. Omit it and dark mode stops working.

## Page-specific vendor files (from the actual pages)

| Page type | Extra head CSS (after vendors.min.css) | Extra JS (after vendors.min.js) | Page init |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dashboard (index.html) | daterangepicker.min.css | daterangepicker.min.js, apexcharts.min.js, circle-progress.min.js | dashboard-init.min.js |
| List/table (customers.html) | dataTables.bs5.min.css, select2.min.css, select2-theme.min.css | dataTables.min.js, dataTables.bs5.min.js, select2.min.js, select2-active.min.js | customers-init.min.js |
| Create/edit form (customers-create.html) | select2.min.css, select2-theme.min.css, datepicker.min.css | select2.min.js, select2-active.min.js, datepicker.min.js, lslstrength.min.js | customers-create-init.min.js |
| Detail/view (customers-view.html) | select2.min.css, select2-theme.min.css | select2.min.js, select2-active.min.js | customers-view-init.min.js |
| Auth (auth-login-minimal.html) | (none) | (none) | (none — only common-init) |

Notes:
- `select2-active.min.js` is what turns `data-select2-selector="status|tag|country|..."`
  selects into colored pills/tags — required wherever those selects appear.
- Every template page has its own `<name>-init.min.js` in `assets/js/` (customers-init,
  leads-init, projects-init, invoice-create-init, apps-*-init, widgets-*-init, settings-init,
  reports-*-init, analytics-init...). These are tiny: e.g. customers-init.min.js just does
  `$("#customerList").DataTable({pageLength:10,lengthMenu:[10,20,50,100,200,500]})` plus
  check-all-checkbox wiring. For generated screens, write equivalent small init snippets.
- HTMX caveat: all init scripts run on `$(document).ready` — content swapped in via HTMX will
  NOT be initialized (DataTables, select2, tooltips). Re-run the relevant init on
  `htmx:afterSwap` or scope initialization to the swapped node.

## Shared vs page-specific init

- Shared (every page): `assets/js/common-init.min.js` — sidebar mini/expand (`minimenu` class
  on `<html>`), mega-menu open/close, page-header-right mobile offcanvas toggles, tooltip init,
  responsiv

# Lightweight HTMX and PHP Design Patterns with PostgreSQL and PDO

> The owner's canonical pattern document, preserved in full. Where an example here differs from a locked plugin rule (design-system markup, the CRUD contract, the no-modal rule, HTMX CSRF headers), SKILL.md's "Plugin integration" section states the reconciliation — the structure and layering below always stand.

## Recommended Pattern

Use a **feature-oriented Page Controller pattern** with:

* Procedural PHP endpoints as controllers
* Small query functions that accept a `PDO` connection
* Full-page templates for initial navigation
* Partial templates for HTMX responses
* Shared helpers for database access, rendering, escaping, CSRF protection, and HTMX detection

This is effectively a lightweight MVC structure:

| Responsibility | Implementation |
| --- | --- |
| Controller | Small PHP endpoint |
| Data access | Procedural functions using PDO |
| Full-page view | `page.php` |
| Fragment view | Files under `partials/` |
| Application infrastructure | `bootstrap.php`, `db.php`, and `http.php` |

HTMX works particularly well with this structure because the server returns HTML rather than JSON, and HTMX inserts that HTML into the specified target.

Avoid introducing controllers, repositories, services, DTOs, or entity classes initially. Extract reusable components only when duplicated business logic becomes substantial.

## 1. Directory Structure

```text
project/
├── app/
│   ├── bootstrap.php
│   ├── db.php
│   ├── http.php
│   │
│   ├── features/
│   │   └── customers/
│   │       └── queries.php
│   │
│   └── views/
│       ├── layout.php
│       │
│       ├── shared/
│       │   ├── flash.php
│       │   └── validation-errors.php
│       │
│       └── customers/
│           ├── page.php
│           └── partials/
│               ├── table.php
│               ├── row.php
│               ├── form.php
│               └── saved.php
│
├── public/
│   ├── index.php
│   │
│   └── customers/
│       ├── index.php
│       ├── form.php
│       ├── save.php
│       └── delete.php
│
└── config/
    └── application.php
```

This is a **vertical-slice structure**: everything related to the customer interface is grouped under `customers`, while executable endpoints remain under `public`. `public/customers/save.php` is callable through HTTP; `app/views/customers/partials/form.php` is included by PHP and must not be directly accessible through the web server.

## 2. HTMX + PHP Patterns

**Pattern A — Dedicated fragment endpoints.** An endpoint that always returns one specific HTML fragment (`hx-get="/customers/list.php"` on a container, often with `hx-trigger="load"`). Use for dropdown options, search results, table bodies, paginated lists, dashboard widgets. Easy to understand; can multiply small endpoint files.

**Pattern B — Dual full-page and partial endpoint.** The same URL returns a complete page for normal navigation or a fragment for an HTMX request, detected via the `HX-Request: true` header:

```php
if (is_htmx_request()) {
    header('Vary: HX-Request');
    echo $tableHtml;
    exit;
}
echo view('layout.php', ['content' => $pageHtml]);
```

`Vary: HX-Request` prevents HTTP caches from confusing the two responses. Use for searchable index pages, pagination, filtered reports, sortable tables, tabbed interfaces. Keeps URLs usable without JavaScript.

**Pattern C — Replace the nearest component.** A control inside a row operates on that row:

```html
<button type="button"
    hx-post="/customers/delete.php"
    hx-vals='{"id": 42}'
    hx-target="closest tr"
    hx-swap="delete"
    hx-confirm="Delete this customer?">
    Delete
</button>
```

`closest tr` keeps the interface independent of generated element IDs. Use for deleting rows, replacing cards, toggling status, inline editing, expanding detail sections.

**Pattern D — Server-triggered refresh.** After a save, the server emits an event; interested regions listen:

```php
header('HX-Trigger: customersChanged');

```

3. The server-side guard accepts the token from the `X-CSRF-Token` header **or** the hidden form field, on all unsafe verbs, compared with `hash_equals`.

## Beyond passwords (plugin additions)

- [references/google-signin.md](references/google-signin.md) — "Sign in with Google" via server-side OIDC: flow, ID-token validation, the `auth_identities` table, account-linking rules, and the nullable-`password_hash` consequence.
- [references/totp-2fa.md](references/totp-2fa.md) — authenticator-app 2FA: enrollment, the pending-2FA login state, recovery codes, replay/rate-limit rules.

Both paths converge on the same session establishment: `session_regenerate_id(true)`, set `user_id`, mint a fresh CSRF token — no method skips it, and enabled 2FA applies to Google sign-ins exactly as to passwords.
=== GOOGLE SIGNIN (full) ===
# Sign in with Google (server-side OIDC)

Every application's login page offers "Sign in with Google" alongside email/password. Use the **server-side OAuth 2.0 / OIDC authorization-code redirect flow** — not the Google JS button or One Tap (those inject third-party JS and popup/iframe UI, which fights the no-modal rule and CSP). The login button is a plain link/button to `/auth/google/start`; everything else is PHP.

Library: `composer require league/oauth2-client league/oauth2-google` (light, maintained). Hand-rolling with curl + JWKS verification is acceptable but not the default.

## Flow

1. `/auth/google/start` — generate `state` (session-bound random) and PKCE verifier/challenge, store both in the session, redirect to Google's authorization endpoint with `scope=openid email profile`, the challenge, and a `nonce`.
2. `/auth/google/callback` — verify `state` with `hash_equals` (this is the CSRF guard for the OAuth flow); exchange the code (with the PKCE verifier) for tokens; validate the **ID token**: signature against Google's JWKS, `iss` (`https://accounts.google.com`), `aud` (your client id), `exp`, and the `nonce`. The league provider handles most of this — never trust claims without it.
3. **Require `email_verified: true`.** An unverified Google email is treated as no email: refuse with a generic error.
4. Resolve the account (below), then establish the session exactly like password login: `session_regenerate_id(true)`, set `user_id`, mint a fresh CSRF token, write the activity-log row (`login`, method `google`). **If the account has 2FA enabled, do not establish the session yet** — enter the pending-2FA state per `totp-2fa.md`; Google sign-in does not bypass 2FA.

## Account model

```sql
CREATE TABLE auth_identities (
    id                bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id           bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    provider          text   NOT NULL,             -- 'google'
    provider_user_id  text   NOT NULL,             -- Google's stable 'sub' claim
    email_at_provider text   NOT NULL,
    created_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (provider, provider_user_id)
);
```

`users.password_hash` becomes **nullable** — a Google-only account has none. A null hash means password login is simply impossible for that account (the dummy-hash verify path handles it without enumeration leaks); adding a password later goes through the email-verified reset flow, never a plain form.

## Linking rules (fixed policy)

1. Match `(provider='google', provider_user_id=sub)` → log that user in. The `sub` claim is the identity key; the email is display data (Google emails can change).
2. No identity match, but a user exists with the same normalized, Google-verified email → 
```

# MaluMail Send API

Send transactional email over a small REST API. This is the **live** contract at
`api.malumail.com` — every field, status code, and behavior below matches the
running service. Do not invent fields or endpoints beyond what is listed here.

- **Base URL:** `https://api.malumail.com`  (HTTPS only; non-HTTPS → `400`)
- **Auth:** `Authorization: Bearer <api-key>` on every request
- **Bodies/responses:** JSON

## Prerequisites (one-time, in the portal at https://malumail.com/portal/)

1. **Verified sending domain.** You may only send `from` an address whose domain
   is verified on the account (ownership TXT + `SPF include:_spf.malumail.com` +
   DKIM published). Unverified `from` domain → `403`. DKIM/SPF are then applied
   automatically; you never sign anything.
2. **API key.** Portal → **API Keys** → create. Shown **once**, unrecoverable.
   Format `mm_` + 48 lowercase hex chars (regex `^mm_[a-f0-9]{48}$`). Store as a
   secret; never commit it. Revoking in the portal makes it `401` immediately.

## Send an email — `POST /v1/send`

Request JSON:

| Field       | Type               | Required | Notes |
|-------------|--------------------|----------|-------|
| `from`      | string             | yes | Valid email; its domain must be a verified sending domain (else `403`). |
| `from_name` | string             | no  | Display name → `"Name" <from>`. |
| `to`        | string \| string[] | yes | 1–50 addresses. All go in **one shared message** (recipients see each other). |
| `subject`   | string             | yes | Non-empty. |
| `text`      | string             | one of | Plain-text body. |
| `html`      | string             | one of | HTML body. |

At least one of `text`/`html` is required. Both → `multipart/alternative`.
Server auto-generates `Date`, `Message-ID`, `MIME-Version`.

Success `200`:

```json
{"status":"sent","accepted":["alice@example.com"],
 "rejected":[{"email":"bob@example.com","reason":"suppressed:bounce"},
             {"email":"bad","reason":"invalid_address"}]}
```

- **Partial success is still `200`** — always inspect `rejected`.
- `rejected` reasons: `invalid_address`, or `suppressed:<reason>` (never delivered).
- If **all** recipients are rejected → `400 {"error":"No deliverable recipients.","rejected":[...]}`.

Example:

```bash
curl -sS -X POST https://api.malumail.com/v1/send \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $MALUMAIL_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"from":"noreply@yourdomain.com","from_name":"Your App",
       "to":"alice@example.com","subject":"Welcome",
       "text":"Thanks for signing up.","html":"<p>Thanks for signing up.</p>"}'
```

PHP (the plugin-standard helper — `app/mail.php`, loaded by `bootstrap.php`, per php-patterns):

```php
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

/**
 * Send one email via MaluMail. Returns the decoded API response.
 * Throws RuntimeException on transport errors and non-2xx responses.
 */
function malumail_send(array $mail): array
{
    $ch = curl_init('https://api.malumail.com/v1/send');
    curl_setopt_array($ch, [
        CURLOPT_POST           => true,
        CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
        CURLOPT_TIMEOUT        => 30,
        CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER     => [
            'A
```

  In the PHP build this stops being a concern: routes are root-relative (`/customers`,

($_SERVER['HTTP_X_CSRF_TOKEN'] ?? '');\n    if (!hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf_token'] ?? '', (string) $sent)) {\n        http_response_code(403);\n        exit('CSRF validation failed.');\n    }\n}\n\nfunction current_user_id(): ?int\n{\n    $id = $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null;\n    return is_int($id) ? $id : null;\n}\n\nfunction current_user(): ?array\n{\n    static $user = false;\n    if ($user !== false) {\n        return $user;\n    }\n    $id = current_user_id();\n    if ($id === null) {\n        return $user = null;\n    }\n    $stmt = db()->prepare('SELECT id, email, display_name, role, totp_enabled_at FROM users WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $user = ($row === false ? null : $row);\n}\n\nfunction require_login(): array\n{\n    $user = current_user();\n    if ($user === null) {\n        redirect('/login');\n    }\n    return $user;\n}\n\nfunction require_admin(): array\n{\n    $user = require_login();\n    if ($user['role'] !== 'admin') {\n        http_response_code(403);\n        exit('Forbidden');\n    }\n    return $user;\n}\n\n/** Establish the session after ANY successful primary+secondary auth. */\nfunction establish_session(int $userId): void\n{\n    session_regenerate_id(true);\n    $_SESSION['user_id'] = $userId;\n    unset($_SESSION['pending_2fa_user_id'], $_SESSION['pending_2fa_started_at']);\n    unset($_SESSION['csrf_token']);   // mint fresh post-login\n    csrf_token();\n}\n\n// --- Brute-force throttle (php-session-auth non-negotiable) ---------------\n\nfunction too_many_attempts(PDO $pdo, ?string $email, string $ip, string $kind = 'password'): bool\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT\n          count(*) FILTER (WHERE email = :email AND NOT succeeded) AS by_email,\n          count(*) FILTER (WHERE ip = :ip AND NOT succeeded)       AS by_ip\n        FROM login_attempts\n        WHERE kind = :kind AND attempted_at > now() - interval '15 minutes'\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['email' => $email ?? '', 'ip' => $ip, 'kind' => $kind]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    return ((int) $row['by_email']) >= 5 || ((int) $row['by_ip']) >= 20;\n}\n\nfunction record_attempt(PDO $pdo, ?string $email, string $ip, string $kind, bool $succeeded): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO login_attempts (email, ip, kind, succeeded)\n        VALUES (:email, :ip, :kind, :succeeded)\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute([\n        'email' => $email, 'ip' => $ip, 'kind' => $kind,\n        'succeeded' => $succeeded ? 't' : 'f',\n    ]);\n}\n\nfunction client_ip(): string\n{\n    return $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '0.0.0.0';\n}\n\n/** Dummy hash precomputed at PASSWORD_DEFAULT's current cost — timing-equalized verify. */\nfunction dummy_password_hash(): string\n{\n    static $hash = null;\n    return $hash ??= password_hash('timing-equalizer-not-a-real-password', PASSWORD_DEFAULT);\n}\nEOF\n\ncat > app/log.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\n/**\n * Activity memory emission point — every screen entry, record change, and game\n * action writes a row (new-app skill: logging is not optional).\n * $opts: actor_type, actor_id, actor_label, screen, entity, entity_id, game_id,\n *        before, after, details (arrays are JSON-encoded).\n */\nfunction log_activity(PDO $pdo, string $action, array $opts = []): void\n{\n    $actorType  = $opts['actor_type'] ?? null;\n    $actorId    = $opts['actor_id'] ?? null;\n    $actorLabel = $opts['actor_label'] ?? null;\n\n    if ($actorType === null) {\n        $user = function_exists('current_user') ? current_user() : null;\n        if ($user !== null) {\n            $actorType  = 'user';\n            $actorId    = (int) $user['id'];\n            $actorLabel = $user['display_name'];\n        } else {\n            $actorType = 'system';\n        }\n    }\n\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO activity_log\n            (actor_type, actor_id, actor_label, action, screen, entity, entity_id,\n             game_id, before_data, after_data, details, ip)\n        VALUES\n            (:actor_type, :actor_id, :actor_label, :action, :screen, :entity, :entity_id,\n             :game_id, :before_data, :after_data, :details, :ip)\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute([\n        'actor_type'  => $actorType,\n        'actor_id'    => $actorId,\n        'actor_label' => $actorLabel ?? '',\n        'action'      => $action,\n        'screen'      => $opts['screen'] ?? '',\n        'entity'      => $opts['entity'] ?? '',\n        'entity_id'   => $opts['entity_id'] ?? null,\n        'game_id'     => $opts['game_id'] ?? null,\n        'before_data' => isset($opts['before']) ? json_encode($opts['before']) : null,\n        'after_data'  => isset($opts['after']) ? json_encode($opts['after']) : null,\n        'details'     => isset($opts['details']) ? json_encode($opts['details']) : null,\n        'ip'          => client_ip(),\n    ]);\n}\n\nfunction log_screen_view(PDO $pdo, string $screen): void\n{\n    log_activity($pdo, 'screen_view', ['screen' => $screen]);\n}\nEOF\necho OK",

Returns the decoded API response.\n * Throws RuntimeException on transport errors and non-2xx responses.\n */\nfunction malumail_send(array $mail): array\n{\n    $ch = curl_init('https://api.malumail.com/v1/send');\n    curl_setopt_array($ch, [\n        CURLOPT_POST           => true,\n        CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\n        CURLOPT_TIMEOUT        => 30,\n        CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER     => [\n            'Authorization: Bearer ' . config('MALUMAIL_API_KEY'),\n            'Content-Type: application/json',\n        ],\n        CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS     => json_encode($mail, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR),\n    ]);\n    $body   = curl_exec($ch);\n    $status = curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE);\n    $errno  = curl_errno($ch);\n    curl_close($ch);\n\n    if ($errno !== 0 || $body === false) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('MaluMail transport error.');\n    }\n    $decoded = json_decode((string) $body, true);\n    if ($status !== 200) {\n        throw new RuntimeException(\"MaluMail send failed ({$status}): \" . ($decoded['error'] ?? 'unknown'));\n    }\n    return $decoded;\n}\n\n/**\n * App-level send. Without a MALUMAIL_API_KEY (local dev) the message is written\n * to storage/mail.log instead so reset/verification flows stay testable.\n */\nfunction send_app_mail(string $to, string $subject, string $text, string $html = ''): void\n{\n    if (config('MALUMAIL_API_KEY') === '') {\n        $dir = dirname(__DIR__) . '/storage';\n        if (!is_dir($dir)) { mkdir($dir, 0775, true); }\n        file_put_contents(\n            $dir . '/mail.log',\n            sprintf(\"[%s] To: %s\\nSubject: %s\\n%s\\n\\n\", date('c'), $to, $subject, $text),\n            FILE_APPEND\n        );\n        return;\n    }\n    malumail_send([\n        'from'      => config('MAIL_FROM', 'noreply@example.com'),\n        'from_name' => config('APP_NAME', 'Cert Arena'),\n        'to'        => $to,\n        'subject'   => $subject,\n        'text'      => $text,\n        'html'      => $html !== '' ? $html : null,\n    ]);\n}\nEOF\n\ncat > app/totp.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\n// Authenticator-app TOTP (RFC 6238, SHA-1, 6 digits, 30s) — no dependency needed.\n\nfunction totp_generate_secret(): string\n{\n    $alphabet = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567';\n    $secret = '';\n    for ($i = 0; $i < 32; $i++) {\n        $secret .= $alphabet[random_int(0, 31)];\n    }\n    return $secret;\n}\n\nfunction base32_decode_totp(string $b32): string\n{\n    $alphabet = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567';\n    $b32 = strtoupper(rtrim($b32, '='));\n    $bits = '';\n    foreach (str_split($b32) as $c) {\n        $v = strpos($alphabet, $c);\n        if ($v === false) { throw new InvalidArgumentException('Bad base32.'); }\n        $bits .= str_pad(decbin($v), 5, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);\n    }\n    $out = '';\n    foreach (str_split($bits, 8) as $byte) {\n        if (strlen($byte) === 8) { $out .= chr((int) bindec($byte)); }\n    }\n    return $out;\n}\n\nfunction totp_code(string $secret, int $timestep): string\n{\n    $key  = base32_decode_totp($secret);\n    $data = pack('N*', 0, $timestep);\n    $hash = hash_hmac('sha1', $data, $key, true);\n    $offset = ord($hash[19]) & 0x0f;\n    $code = (\n        ((ord($hash[$offset]) & 0x7f) << 24) |\n        ((ord($hash[$offset + 1]) & 0xff) << 16) |\n        ((ord($hash[$offset + 2]) & 0xff) << 8) |\n        (ord($hash[$offset + 3]) & 0xff)\n    ) % 1000000;\n    return str_pad((string) $code, 6, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);\n}\n\n/**\n * Verify with a ±1 timestep window. Returns the accepted timestep (for the\n * replay guard: reject any timestep <= users.totp_last_timestep), or null.\n */\nfunction totp_verify(string $secret, string $code, int $lastTimestep): ?int\n{\n    $now = intdiv(time(), 30);\n    foreach ([$now, $now - 1, $now + 1] as $step) {\n        if ($step > $lastTimestep && hash_equals(totp_code($secret, $step), $code)) {\n            return $step;\n        }\n    }\n    return null;\n}\n\n// Secrets encrypted at rest (libsodium secretbox, key in env — totp-2fa.md).\n\nfunction totp_encrypt_secret(string $secret): string\n{\n    $key = hex2bin(config('TOTP_ENC_KEY'));\n    if ($key === false || strlen($key) !== SODIUM_CRYPTO_SECRETBOX_KEYBYTES) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('TOTP_ENC_KEY must be 64 hex chars.');\n    }\n    $nonce = random_bytes(SODIUM_CRYPTO_SECRETBOX_NONCEBYTES);\n    return base64_encode($nonce . sodium_crypto_secretbox($secret, $nonce, $key));\n}\n\nfunction totp_decrypt_secret(string $stored): string\n{\n    $key = hex2bin(config('TOTP_ENC_KEY'));\n    $raw = base64_decode($stored, true);\n    if ($key === false || $raw === false) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('TOTP secret decryption failed.');\n    }\n    $nonce = substr($raw, 0, SODIUM_CRYPTO_SECRETBOX_NONCEBYTES);\n    $box   = substr($raw, SODIUM_CRYPTO_SECRETBOX_NONCEBYTES);\n    $plain = sodium_crypto_secretbox_open($box, $nonce, $key);\n    if ($plain === false) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('TOTP secret decryption failed.');\n    }\n    return $plain;\n}\nEOF\n\ncat > app/bootstrap.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/config.php';\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/db.php';\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/http.php';\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/auth.php';\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/log.php';\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/mail.php';\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/totp.php';\n\n// Session bootstrap per php-session-auth (before session_start()).\n$isHttps = (($_SERVER['HTTPS'] ?? '') !== '' && $_SERVER['HTTPS'] !== 'off')\n    || (($_SERVER['HTTP_X_FORWARDED_PROTO'] ?? '') === 'https');\nsession_set_cookie_params([\n    'lifetime' => 0, 'path' => '/', 'secure' => $isHttps,\n    'httponly' => true, 'samesite' => 'Lax',\n]);\nini_set('session.use_strict_mode', '1');\nini_set('session.use_only_cookies', '1');\nsession_name('SID');\nsession_start();\nEOF\n\ncat > config/env.example.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\n// Copy to config/env.php (gitignored) and fill in. Production uses real env vars.\nputenv('APP_ENV=dev');                 // dev | production\nputenv('APP_NAME=Cert Arena');\nputenv('APP_URL=http://localhost:8080');\nputenv('DB_HOST=127.0.0.1');\nputenv('DB_PORT=5432');\nputenv('DB_NAME=studygame');\nputenv('DB_USER=');                    // dev default: current OS user\nputenv('DB_PASSWORD=');\nputenv('TOTP_ENC_KEY=');               // 64 hex chars: php -r \"echo bin2hex(random_bytes(32));\"\nputenv('MALUMAIL_API_KEY=');           // empty in dev -> emails land in storage/mail.log\nputenv('MAIL_FROM=noreply@example.com');\nputenv('GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=');           // empty -> Google button hidden\nputenv('GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=');\nEOF\nphp -r \"echo 'TOTP key: '; echo bin2hex(random_bytes(32)) . PHP_EOL;\" > /tmp/totp_key.txt 2>/dev/null\nKEY=$(php -r \"echo bin2hex(random_bytes(32));\")\nsed \"s|putenv('TOTP_ENC_KEY=');|putenv('TOTP_ENC_KEY=$KEY');|\" config/env.example.php > config/env.php\necho OK; php -l app/bootstrap.php && php -l app/auth.php && php -l app/totp.php && php -l app/mail.php",

{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\n\ncat >> app/http.php << 'EOF'\n\n// --- Flash messages (one-shot, session-backed) ----------------------------\n\nfunction flash_set(string $kind, string $message): void\n{\n    $_SESSION['flash'] = ['kind' => $kind, 'message' => $message];\n}\n\nfunction flash_take(): ?array\n{\n    $flash = $_SESSION['flash'] ?? null;\n    unset($_SESSION['flash']);\n    return $flash;\n}\nEOF\n\ncat > app/features/auth/queries.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nfunction count_users(PDO $pdo): int\n{\n    return (int) $pdo->query('SELECT count(*) FROM users')->fetchColumn();\n}\n\nfunction find_user_by_email(PDO $pdo, string $email): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = :email LIMIT 1');\n    $stmt->execute(['email' => $email]);\n    $user = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $user === false ? null : $user;\n}\n\nfunction find_user(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $user = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $user === false ? null : $user;\n}\n\n/** First registered user becomes admin (bootstrap rule for the study group). */\nfunction insert_user(PDO $pdo, string $email, string $displayName, ?string $passwordHash, bool $verified): array\n{\n    $role = count_users($pdo) === 0 ? 'admin' : 'member';\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO users (email, display_name, password_hash, role, email_verified_at)\n        VALUES (:email, :display_name, :password_hash, :role, :verified_at)\n        RETURNING id, email, display_name, role\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute([\n        'email' => $email, 'display_name' => $displayName,\n        'password_hash' => $passwordHash, 'role' => $role,\n        'verified_at' => $verified ? date('c') : null,\n    ]);\n    return $stmt->fetch();\n}\n\nfunction set_user_password(PDO $pdo, int $userId, string $hash): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('UPDATE users SET password_hash = :hash WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['hash' => $hash, 'id' => $userId]);\n}\n\nfunction mark_email_verified(PDO $pdo, int $userId): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('UPDATE users SET email_verified_at = now() WHERE id = :id AND email_verified_at IS NULL');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $userId]);\n}\n\n// --- External identities (google-signin.md) -------------------------------\n\nfunction find_identity(PDO $pdo, string $provider, string $providerUserId): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT * FROM auth_identities\n        WHERE provider = :provider AND provider_user_id = :sub LIMIT 1\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['provider' => $provider, 'sub' => $providerUserId]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $row === false ? null : $row;\n}\n\nfunction user_has_identity(PDO $pdo, int $userId, string $provider): bool\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT 1 FROM auth_identities WHERE user_id = :id AND provider = :provider LIMIT 1');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $userId, 'provider' => $provider]);\n    return $stmt->fetch() !== false;\n}\n\nfunction insert_identity(PDO $pdo, int $userId, string $provider, string $providerUserId, string $email): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO auth_identities (user_id, provider, provider_user_id, email_at_provider)\n        VALUES (:user_id, :provider, :sub, :email)\n        RETURNING id\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['user_id' => $userId, 'provider' => $provider, 'sub' => $providerUserId, 'email' => $email]);\n    return $stmt->fetch();\n}\n\n// --- One-time tokens (reset + verify) -------------------------------------\n\nfunction insert_user_token(PDO $pdo, int $userId, string $purpose, string $tokenHash, string $expiresInterval): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO user_tokens (user_id, purpose, token_hash, expires_at)\n        VALUES (:user_id, :purpose, :hash, now() + :expires::interval)\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['user_id' => $userId, 'purpose' => $purpose, 'hash' => $tokenHash, 'expires' => $expiresInterval]);\n}\n\nfunction find_valid_user_token(PDO $pdo, string $purpose, string $tokenHash): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT * FROM user_tokens\n        WHERE purpose = :purpose AND token_hash = :hash\n          AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now()\n        LIMIT 1\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['purpose' => $purpose, 'hash' => $tokenHash]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $row === false ? null : $row;\n}\n\nfunction consume_user_token(PDO $pdo, int $tokenId): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('UPDATE user_tokens SET used_at = now() WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $tokenId]);\n}\n\n// --- TOTP 2FA (totp-2fa.md) ------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction enable_user_totp(PDO $pdo, int $userId, string $encryptedSecret): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        UPDATE users SET totp_secret = :secret, totp_enabled_at = now(), totp_last_timestep = 0\n        WHERE id = :id\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['secret' => $encryptedSecret, 'id' => $userId]);\n}\n\nfunction disable_user_totp(PDO $pdo, int $userId): void\n{\n    $pdo->prepare('UPDATE users SET totp_secret = NULL, totp_enabled_at = NULL, totp_last_timestep = NULL WHERE id = :id')\n        ->execute(['id' => $userId]);\n    $pdo->prepare('DELETE FROM totp_recovery_codes WHERE user_id = :id')\n        ->execute(['id' => $userId]);\n}\n\nfunction store_totp_timestep(PDO $pdo, int $userId, int $timestep): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('UPDATE users SET totp_last_timestep = :step WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['step' => $timestep, 'id' => $userId]);\n}\n\nfunction insert_recovery_codes(PDO $pdo, int $userId, array $hashes): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('INSERT INTO totp_recovery_codes (user_id, code_hash) VALUES (:id, :hash)');\n    foreach ($hashes as $hash) {\n        $stmt->execute(['id' => $userId, 'hash' => $hash]);\n    }\n}\n\nfunction find_unused_recovery_codes(PDO $pdo, int $userId): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id, code_hash FROM totp_recovery_codes WHERE user_id = :id AND used_at IS NULL');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $userId]);\n    return $stmt->fetchAll();\n}\n\nfunction mark_recovery_code_used(PDO $pdo, int $codeId): void\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('UPDATE totp_recovery_codes SET used_at = now() WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $codeId]);\n}\nEOF\nphp -l app/features/auth/queries.php",
  "description": "Write auth query functions"
}

'');\n$row    = $token === '' ? null : find_valid_user_token($pdo, 'password_reset', hash('sha256', $token));\n\nif ($row === null) {\n    flash_set('warning', 'That reset link is invalid or expired. Request a new one.');\n    header('Location: /password/forgot');\n    exit;\n}\n\nif ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {\n    verify_csrf();\n    $password = (string) ($_POST['password'] ?? '');\n    if (mb_strlen($password) < 12) { $errors[] = 'Password must be at least 12 characters.'; }\n    if (strlen($password) > 72)    { $errors[] = 'Password must be at most 72 bytes.'; }\n\n    if ($errors === []) {\n        consume_user_token($pdo, (int) $row['id']);\n        set_user_password($pdo, (int) $row['user_id'], password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT));\n        mark_email_verified($pdo, (int) $row['user_id']);   // reset proves mailbox control\n        log_activity($pdo, 'password_reset_completed', [\n            'actor_type' => 'user', 'actor_id' => (int) $row['user_id'],\n            'screen' => 'password-reset', 'entity' => 'users', 'entity_id' => (int) $row['user_id'],\n        ]);\n        flash_set('success', 'Password updated. Log in with your new password.');\n        header('Location: /login');\n        exit;\n    }\n}\n\n$content = view('auth/reset.php', ['token' => $token, 'errors' => $errors]);\necho view('auth/layout.php', ['title' => 'Choose a new password', 'content' => $content]);\nEOF\n\ncat > public/login/twofa.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/auth/queries.php';\n\nauth_page_headers();\n\n$pendingId = $_SESSION['pending_2fa_user_id'] ?? null;\n$startedAt = $_SESSION['pending_2fa_started_at'] ?? 0;\nif (!is_int($pendingId) || (time() - (int) $startedAt) > 600) {\n    unset($_SESSION['pending_2fa_user_id'], $_SESSION['pending_2fa_started_at']);\n    header('Location: /login');\n    exit;\n}\n\n$pdo   = db();\n$user  = find_user($pdo, $pendingId);\n$error = null;\nif ($user === null || $user['totp_enabled_at'] === null) {\n    header('Location: /login');\n    exit;\n}\n\nif ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {\n    verify_csrf();\n    $code = preg_replace('/\\s+/', '', (string) ($_POST['code'] ?? ''));\n\n    if (too_many_attempts($pdo, $user['email'], client_ip(), 'totp')) {\n        http_response_code(429);\n        $error = 'Too many attempts. Try again later.';\n    } else {\n        $ok = false;\n        if (preg_match('/^\\d{6}$/', $code) === 1) {\n            $step = totp_verify(totp_decrypt_secret($user['totp_secret']), $code, (int) ($user['totp_last_timestep'] ?? 0));\n            if ($step !== null) {\n                store_totp_timestep($pdo, $pendingId, $step);\n                $ok = true;\n            }\n        } else {\n            foreach (find_unused_recovery_codes($pdo, $pendingId) as $recovery) {\n                if (password_verify($code, $recovery['code_hash'])) {\n                    mark_recovery_code_used($pdo, (int) $recovery['id']);\n                    $ok = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        record_attempt($pdo, $user['email'], client_ip(), 'totp', $ok);\n\n        if ($ok) {\n            establish_session($pendingId);\n            log_activity($pdo, 'login_2fa', ['screen' => 'login-2fa']);\n            header('Location: /');\n            exit;\n        }\n        $error = 'Invalid code.';\n    }\n}\n\n$content = view('auth/twofa.php', ['error' => $error]);\necho view('auth/layout.php', ['title' => 'Two-factor authentication', 'content' => $content]);\nEOF\nfor f in public/login.php public/logout.php public/register.php public/verify.php public/password/forgot.php public/password/reset.php public/login/twofa.php; do php -l $f | grep -v \"No syntax\"; done; echo \"lint done\"",

              </button>\n                            </form>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n        </div>\n    </header>\n [End] Header !-->\n    <!--! [Start] Main !-->\n    <main class=\"nxl-container\">\n        <div class=\"nxl-content\" id=\"page-content\">\n<?= $content ?>\n        </div>\n        <footer class=\"footer pb-5\" id=\"page-footer\">\n            <p class=\"fs-11 text-muted fw-medium text-uppercase mb-0 copyright\">\n                <span>Copyright © <?= date('Y') ?> — <?= e(config('APP_NAME', 'Cert Arena')) ?></span>\n            </p>\n        </footer>\n    </main>\n    <!--! [Start] Assistant command bar (chat-actions; stub handler until Phase 4) !-->\n    <div id=\"assistant-bar\" class=\"fixed-bottom bg-body border-top p-2\">\n        <div class=\"container-fluid\">\n            <div id=\"assistant-reply\" class=\"fs-12 text-muted mb-1\"></div>\n            <form id=\"assistant-form\"\n                  hx-post=\"/assistant/message\" hx-target=\"#assistant-reply\" hx-swap=\"innerHTML\"\n                  hx-vals='js:{screen: document.querySelector(\"#page-content [data-screen]\")?.dataset.screen || \"\"}'>\n                <div class=\"input-group input-group-sm\">\n                    <span class=\"input-group-text\"><i class=\"feather-message-circle\"></i></span>\n                    <input type=\"text\" name=\"message\" id=\"assistant-input\" class=\"form-control\"\n                           placeholder=\"Ask or tell the assistant… (Ctrl+K)\" autocomplete=\"off\">\n                    <button type=\"submit\" id=\"assistant-send-btn\" class=\"btn btn-primary\">\n                        <i class=\"feather-send\"></i>\n                    </button>\n                </div>\n            </form>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n    <!--! Store them somewhere safe — each one can replace a code if you lose your authenticator.</div>\n                        <div class=\"row\" id=\"settings-2fa-recovery-codes\">\n                            <?php foreach ($recoveryCodes as $index => $code): ?>\n                                <div class=\"col-6 col-md-4 mb-2\"><code><?= e($code) ?></code></div>\n                            <?php endforeach; ?>\n                        </div>\n                        <a href=\"/settings/2fa\" class=\"btn btn-primary mt-3\" id=\"settings-2fa-done-btn\">Done</a>\n                    </div>\n                <?php else: ?>\n                    <div class=\"card-header\">\n                        <h5 class=\"card-title\">Two-factor authentication is on</h5>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"card-body\">\n                        <p class=\"fs-12 text-muted\">Enabled <?= e(fmt_date($enabledAt)) ?>. Every login — password or Google — asks for a code.</p>\n                        <h6 class=\"fs-13 fw-bold mt-4\">Disable 2FA</h6>\n                        <p class=\"fs-12 text-muted\">Requires a current authenticator code (or a recovery code).</p>\n                        <form action=\"/settings/2fa\" method=\"post\" id=\"settings-2fa-disable-form\" class=\"row g-2\">\n                            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"<?= e(csrf_token()) ?>\">\n                            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"do\" value=\"disable\">\n                            <div class=\"col-auto\">\n                                <input type=\"text\" name=\"code\" id=\"settings-2fa-field-disable-code\" class=\"form-control\" placeholder=\"Code\" autocomplete=\"one-time-code\" required>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"col-auto\">\n                                <button type=\"submit\" id=\"settings-2fa-disable-btn\" class=\"btn btn-danger\"\n                                        hx-confirm=\"Disable two-factor authentication?\">Disable</button>\n                            </div>\n                        </form>\n                    </div>\n                <?php endif; ?>\n            </div>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n</div>\n<!-- [ Main Content ] end -->\nEOF\n\ncat > public/settings/twofa.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/auth/queries.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/vendor/autoload.php';\n\nuse chillerlan\\QRCode\\QRCode;\n\n$user = require_login();\n$pdo  = db();\n$errors = [];\n$view   = null;\n\n$full = find_user($pdo, (int) $user['id']);\n$isEnabled = $full['totp_enabled_at'] !== null;\n\nif ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {\n    verify_csrf();\n    $do   = (string) ($_POST['do'] ?? '');\n    $code = preg_replace('/\\s+/', '', (string) ($_POST['code'] ?? ''));\n\n    if ($do === 'enable' && !$isEnabled) {\n        $secret = (string) ($_SESSION['totp_setup_secret'] ?? '');\n        $step   = $secret === '' ? null : totp_verify($secret, $code, 0);\n        if ($step === null) {\n            $errors[] = 'That code didn\\'t match. Scan the QR again and enter a fresh code.';\n        } else {\n            $recoveryCodes = [];\n            $hashes = [];\n            for ($i = 0; $i < 10; $i++) {\n                $plain = strtolower(bin2hex(random_bytes(5)));           // 10 hex chars\n                $recoveryCodes[] = substr($plain, 0, 5) . '-' . substr($plain, 5);\n                $hashes[] = password_hash(end($recoveryCodes), PASSWORD_DEFAULT);\n            }\n            try {\n                $pdo->beginTransaction();\n                enable_user_totp($pdo, (int) $user['id'], totp_encrypt_secret($secret));\n                store_totp_timestep($pdo, (int) $user['id'], $step);     // burn the enrollment code\n                insert_recovery_codes($pdo, (int) $user['id'], $hashes);\n                log_activity($pdo, 'totp_enabled', ['screen' => 'settings-2fa', 'entity' => 'users', 'entity_id' => (int) $user['id']]);\n                $pdo->commit();\n            } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n                if ($pdo->inTransaction()) { $pdo->rollBack(); }\n                throw $exception;\n            }\n            unset($_SESSION['totp_setup_secret']);\n            $view = ['mode' => 'codes', 'recoveryCodes' => $recoveryCodes];\n        }\n    } elseif ($do === 'disable' && $isEnabled) {\n        if (too_many_attempts($pdo, $full['email'], client_ip(), 'totp')) {\n            http_response_code(429);\n            $errors[] = 'Too many attempts. Try again later.';\n        } else {\n            $ok = false;\n            if (preg_match('/^\\d{6}$/', $code) === 1) {\n                $step = totp_verify(totp_decrypt_secret($full['totp_secret']), $code, (int) ($full['totp_last_timestep'] ?? 0));\n                if ($step !== null) { $ok = true; }\n            } else {\n                foreach (find_unused_recovery_codes($pdo, (int) $user['id']) as $recovery) {\n                    if (password_verify($code, $recovery['code_hash'])) {\n                        mark_recovery_code_used($pdo, (int) $recovery['id']);\n                        $ok = true;\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            record_attempt($pdo, $full['email'], client_ip(), 'totp', $ok);\n            if ($ok) {\n                disable_user_totp($pdo, (int) $user['id']);\n                log_activity($pdo, 'totp_disabled', ['screen' => 'settings-2fa', 'entity' => 'users', 'entity_id' => (int) $user['id']]);\n                $isEnabled = false;\n                $full['totp_enabled_at'] = null;\n            } else {\n                $errors[] = 'Invalid code.';\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nlog_screen_view($pdo, 'settings-2fa');\n\nif ($view === null) {\n    if ($isEnabled) {\n        $view = ['mode' => 'enabled', 'enabledAt' => $full['totp_enabled_at']];\n    } else {\n        // The secret stays in the session, never the database, until confirmed.\n        if (empty($_SESSION['totp_setup_secret'])) {\n            $_SESSION['totp_setup_secret'] = totp_generate_secret();\n        }\n        $secret = $_SESSION['totp_setup_secret'];\n        $issuer = rawurlencode(config('APP_NAME', 'Cert Arena'));\n        $uri = sprintf(\n            'otpauth://totp/%s:%s?secret=%s&issuer=%s&algorithm=SHA1&digits=6&period=30',\n            $issuer, rawurlencode($full['email']), $secret, $issuer\n        );\n        $view = ['mode' => 'setup', 'secret' => $secret, 'qrDataUri' => (new QRCode())->render($uri)];\n    }\n}\n\n$content = view('settings/twofa.php', $view + ['errors' => $errors]);\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    echo $content;\n    exit;\n}\necho view('layout.php', ['title' => 'Two-Factor Auth', 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-settings-2fa', 'user' => $user]);\nEOF\nphp -l public/settings/twofa.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"; php -l app/views/settings/twofa.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"; echo \"lint done\"",

## 🤖 Claude

**Phase 2 is built, verified, and pushed** — the app is running at `http://localhost:8080` in the browser pane for you to click around, and everything is on GitHub at [maludb-ed/study-game](https://github.com/maludb-ed/study-game) (private; two commits).

**To log in:** `edward.honour@gmail.com` / `cert-arena-dev-2026` (dev-only seed in your local database — real members register themselves, and the first registered account on production becomes admin).

What exists and what I verified in the browser:

- **Full auth stack per the plugin's security skill**: email/password login with timing-equalized verification and per-account + per-IP lockout, registration with anti-enumeration behavior, password reset and email verification (MaluMail in production, `storage/mail.log` fallback in dev), Google sign-in (server-side OIDC — hidden until you add client credentials to `config/env.php`), and authenticator-app 2FA with QR enrollment, recovery codes, and a dedicated challenge page. Logout is POST + CSRF. I exercised login, the auth redirect, and the CSRF-protected HTMX flow live.
- **The application shell looks like the finished product**: Duralux sidebar with every planned screen (Question Bank, Game Night, Analytics, AMA, Settings), dark-mode toggle, user menu, and the assistant command bar fixed at the bottom of every screen (`Ctrl+K` focuses it). I tested it — it echoes your utterance and logs it; the real assistant lands in Phase 4. Unbuilt screens show a "arrives with slice S_n_" placeholder so navigation is fully wired.
- **HTMX navigation verified end-to-end**: sidebar links swap `#page-content` and push the canonical URL (`/questions/`), and deep-linking to that URL renders the full page. One note from testing: the sidebar shows icons-only below 1600px viewport width with flyout submenus — that's the theme's designed behavior, not a bug.
- **Mobile check passed at 375px**: zero horizontal scroll, stacked cards, working hamburger drawer, thumb-reachable command bar.
- **Activity memory is live from the first request** — the log already shows logins, screen views, and assistant messages, which is exactly what MaluDB will ingest.

The Phase 2 checkpoint is yours: take a look at the running shell (desktop and mobile) and confirm the look and feel. When you approve, Phase 3 starts with the exemplar slice — the **question bank with ~70 seeded CCAO-F questions**, which is the moment the app becomes genuinely usable for study.

## 🤖 Claude

Phase 3 begins with the exemplar slice — the question bank (S1), built to its [build spec](docs/build-specs/questions.md). Order: query functions → views → endpoints → router → the ~70-question CCAO-F seed → browser verification. First, the data layer:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\nmkdir -p app/features/questions app/features/exams app/views/questions/partials app/views/exams/partials public/questions public/exams\n\ncat > app/features/questions/queries.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nconst QUESTIONS_PAGE_SIZE = 25;\n\n/** List with filters + pagination. Returns ['rows' => [...], 'total' => int]. */\nfunction find_questions(\n    PDO $pdo,\n    int $examId = 0,\n    int $domainId = 0,\n    string $status = '',\n    string $search = '',\n    string $sort = 'updated_at',\n    int $page = 1\n): array {\n    $allowedSort = [\n        'updated_at' => 'q.updated_at DESC',\n        'stem'       => 'q.stem ASC',\n        'difficulty' => \"array_position(ARRAY['easy','medium','hard'], q.difficulty), q.stem\",\n    ];\n    $orderBy = $allowedSort[$sort] ?? $allowedSort['updated_at'];\n\n    $where  = [];\n    $params = [];\n    if ($examId > 0)   { $where[] = 'q.exam_id = :exam_id';     $params['exam_id'] = $examId; }\n    if ($domainId > 0) { $where[] = 'q.domain_id = :domain_id'; $params['domain_id'] = $domainId; }\n    if ($status !== '' && in_array($status, ['draft', 'active', 'retired'], true)) {\n        $where[] = 'q.status = :status';\n        $params['status'] = $status;\n    }\n    if ($search !== '') {\n        $where[] = '(q.stem ILIKE :search OR q.explanation ILIKE :search)';\n        $params['search'] = '%' . $search . '%';\n    }\n    $whereSql = $where === [] ? '' : 'WHERE ' . implode(' AND ', $where);\n\n    $countStmt = $pdo->prepare(\"SELECT count(*) FROM questions q {$whereSql}\");\n    $countStmt->execute($params);\n    $total = (int) $countStmt->fetchColumn();\n\n    $page   = max(1, $page);\n    $offset = ($page - 1) * QUESTIONS_PAGE_SIZE;\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<SQL\n        SELECT q.id, q.stem, q.difficulty, q.status, q.updated_at,\n               e.code AS exam_code, d.name AS domain_name\n        FROM questions q\n        JOIN exams e ON e.id = q.exam_id\n        JOIN domains d ON d.id = q.domain_id\n        {$whereSql}\n        ORDER BY {$orderBy}\n        LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset\n    SQL);\n    foreach ($params as $key => $value) {\n        $stmt->bindValue($key, $value);\n    }\n    $stmt->bindValue('limit', QUESTIONS_PAGE_SIZE, PDO::PARAM_INT);\n    $stmt->bindValue('offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);\n    $stmt->execute();\n\n    return ['rows' => $stmt->fetchAll(), 'total' => $total];\n}\n\n/** One question with its options (ordered) and play stats. */\nfunction find_question(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT q.*, e.code AS exam_code, e.name AS exam_name, d.name AS domain_name,\n               u.display_name AS author_name,\n               (SELECT count(*) FROM game_questions gq\n                 JOIN answers a ON a.game_question_id = gq.id\n                 WHERE gq.question_id = q.id)                            AS times_answered,\n               (SELECT count(*) FROM game_questions gq\n                 JOIN answers a ON a.game_question_id = gq.id\n                 WHERE gq.question_id = q.id AND a.is_correct)           AS times_correct,\n               (SELECT count(*) FROM game_questions gq WHERE gq.question_id = q.id) AS times_drawn\n        FROM questions q\n        JOIN exams e ON e.id = q.exam_id\n        JOIN domains d ON d.id = q.domain_id\n        LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = q.created_by\n        WHERE q.id = :id\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $question = $stmt->fetch();\n    if ($question === false) {\n        return null;\n    }\n    $optStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT id, option_text, is_correct, display_order\n        FROM question_options\n        WHERE question_id = :id\n        ORDER BY display_order\n    SQL);\n    $optStmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $question['options'] = $optStmt->fetchAll();\n    return $question;\n}\n\n/**\n * $options: list of ['text' => string, 'correct' => bool] in display order.\n * Runs in its own transaction (question + options + activity row belong together\n * — the caller passes $logActor for the log row).\n */\nfunction insert_question(\n    PDO $pdo,\n    int $examId,\n    int $domainId,\n    string $stem,\n    array $options,\n    string $explanation,\n    string $difficulty,\n    string $status,\n    string $source,\n    ?int $createdBy\n): array {\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO questions (exam_id, domain_id, stem, explanation, difficulty, status, source, created_by)\n        VALUES (:exam_id, :domain_id, :stem, :explanation, :difficulty, :status, :source, :created_by)\n        RETURNING id, exam_id, domain_id, stem, explanation, difficulty, status, source\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute([\n        'exam_id' => $examId, 'domain_id' => $domainId, 'stem' => $stem,\n        'explanation' => $explanation, 'difficulty' => $difficulty,\n        'status' => $status, 'source' => $source, 'created_by' => $createdBy,\n    ]);\n    $question = $stmt->fetch();\n    replace_question_options($pdo, (int) $question['id'], $options);\n    return $question;\n}\n\n/** Returns ['before' => row, 'after' => row] for undo/logging. */\nfunction update_question(\n    PDO $pdo,\n    int $id,\n    int $examId,\n    int $domainId,\n    string $stem,\n    array $options,\n    string $explanation,\n    string $difficulty,\n    string $status,\n    string $source\n): array {\n    $before = find_question($pdo, $id);\n    if ($before === null) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('Question not found.');\n    }\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        UPDATE questions\n        SET exam_id = :exam_id, domain_id = :domain_id, stem = :stem,\n            explanation = :explanation, difficulty = :difficulty, status = :status, source = :source\n        WHERE id = :id\n        RETURNING id, exam_id, domain_id, stem, explanation, difficulty, status, source\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute([\n        'id' => $id, 'exam_id' => $examId, 'domain_id' => $domainId, 'stem' => $stem,\n        'explanation' => $explanation, 'difficulty' => $difficulty,\n        'status' => $status, 'source' => $source,\n    ]);\n    $after = $stmt->fetch();\n    replace_question_options($pdo, $id, $options);\n    return ['before' => $before, 'after' => $after];\n}\n\n/** Delete-and-reinsert inside the caller's transaction (build spec rule). */\nfunction replace_question_options(PDO $pdo, int $questionId, array $options): void\n{\n    $pdo->prepare('DELETE FROM question_options WHERE question_id = :id')->execute(['id' => $questionId]);\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO question_options (question_id, option_text, is_correct, display_order)\n        VALUES (:question_id, :text, :correct, :display_order)\n    SQL);\n    foreach (array_values($options) as $index => $option) {\n        $stmt->execute([\n            'question_id'   => $questionId,\n            'text'          => $option['text'],\n            'correct'       => $option['correct'] ? 't' : 'f',\n            'display_order' => $index + 1,\n        ]);\n    }\n}\n\nfunction set_question_status(PDO $pdo, int $id, string $status): array\n{\n    if (!in_array($status, ['draft', 'active', 'retired'], true)) {\n        throw new InvalidArgumentException('Bad status.');\n    }\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        UPDATE questions SET status = :status WHERE id = :id\n        RETURNING id, status\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id, 'status' => $status]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    if ($row === false) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('Question not found.');\n    }\n    return $row;\n}\n\n/** Drafts only, and never once the question has been drawn into a game. */\nfunction delete_question(PDO $pdo, int $id): bool\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        DELETE FROM questions q\n        WHERE q.id = :id AND q.status = 'draft'\n          AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM game_questions gq WHERE gq.question_id = q.id)\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    return $stmt->rowCount() === 1;\n}\n\nfunction find_domains_for_exam(PDO $pdo, int $examId): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT id, name, weight_pct FROM domains\n        WHERE exam_id = :exam_id ORDER BY display_order\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['exam_id' => $examId]);\n    return $stmt->fetchAll();\n}\nEOF\n\ncat > app/features/exams/queries.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nfunction find_exams_with_counts(PDO $pdo): array\n{\n    return $pdo->query(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT e.id, e.code, e.name, e.audience, e.official_question_count,\n               e.duration_minutes, e.passing_score, e.price_usd, e.is_active,\n               count(q.id) FILTER (WHERE q.status = 'active') AS active_questions,\n               count(q.id)                                    AS total_questions\n        FROM exams e\n        LEFT JOIN questions q ON q.exam_id = e.id\n        GROUP BY e.id\n        ORDER BY e.code\n    SQL)->fetchAll();\n}\n\nfunction find_exam(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM exams WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $exam = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $exam === false ? null : $exam;\n}\n\n/** Per-domain bank coverage vs the blueprint, for a game of $gameSize questions. */\nfunction find_exam_coverage(PDO $pdo, int $examId, int $gameSize = 10): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT d.id, d.name, d.weight_pct, d.display_order,\n               count(q.id) FILTER (WHERE q.status = 'active') AS active_questions,\n               count(q.id) FILTER (WHERE q.status = 'draft')  AS draft_questions\n        FROM domains d\n        LEFT JOIN questions q ON q.domain_id = d.id\n        WHERE d.exam_id = :exam_id\n        GROUP BY d.id\n        ORDER BY d.display_order\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['exam_id' => $examId]);\n    $rows = $stmt->fetchAll();\n    foreach ($rows as &$row) {\n        $row['target'] = (int) round(((float) $row['weight_pct'] / 100) * $gameSize);\n        $row['short']  = ((int) $row['active_questions']) < $row['target'];\n    }\n    return $rows;\n}\nEOF\nphp -l app/features/questions/queries.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"; php -l app/features/exams/queries.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"; echo \"queries done\"",
  "description": "Write question and exam query functions for slice S1"
}

'active' : '' ?>\">\n                                <a class=\"page-link\" href=\"<?= e($qs($i)) ?>\"\n                                   hx-get=\"<?= e($qs($i)) ?>\" hx-target=\"#question-list-results\" hx-swap=\"outerHTML\"><?= e($i) ?></a>\n                            </li>\n                        <?php endfor; ?>\n                    </ul>\n                </nav>\n            </div>\n        <?php endif; ?>\n    </div>\n</div>\nEOF\n\ncat > app/views/questions/partials/row.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\n/** One list row. [];\n\n            if (!isset($examsByCode[$examCode])) { $itemErrors[] = \"Unknown exam_code '{$examCode}'.\"; }\n            elseif (!isset($examsByCode[$examCode]['domains'][$domain])) { $itemErrors[] = \"Domain does not match the exam blueprint.\"; }\n            if (mb_strlen($stem) < 10 || mb_strlen($stem) > 1000)              { $itemErrors[] = 'Stem must be 10–1000 chars.'; }\n            if (mb_strlen($explanation) < 10 || mb_strlen($explanation) > 2000) { $itemErrors[] = 'Explanation must be 10–2000 chars.'; }\n            if (!in_array($difficulty, ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'], true))       { $itemErrors[] = 'Bad difficulty.'; }\n            $optionRows = [];\n            $correctCount = 0;\n            if (!is_array($options) || count($options) < 2 || count($options) > 6) {\n                $itemErrors[] = 'Provide 2–6 options.';\n            } else {\n                foreach ($options as $option) {\n                    $text = trim((string) ($option['text'] ?? ''));\n                    $isCorrect = ($option['correct'] ?? false) === true;\n                    if ($text === '' || mb_strlen($text) > 300) { $itemErrors[] = 'Every option needs text (max 300 chars).'; break; }\n                    if ($isCorrect) { $correctCount++; }\n                    $optionRows[] = ['text' => $text, 'correct' => $isCorrect];\n                }\n                if ($correctCount !== 1) { $itemErrors[] = 'Exactly one option must be correct.'; }\n            }\n\n            if ($itemErrors !== []) {\n                $report[] = ['index' => $index + 1, 'errors' => $itemErrors];\n            } else {\n                $parsed[] = [\n                    'exam_id'     => $examsByCode[$examCode]['id'],\n                    'domain_id'   => $examsByCode[$examCode]['domains'][$domain],\n                    'stem'        => $stem,\n                    'options'     => $optionRows,\n                    'explanation' => $explanation,\n                    'difficulty'  => $difficulty,\n                    'source'      => trim('import ' . date('Y-m-d') . ': ' . $source),\n                ];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if ($do === 'import' && $report === [] && $parsed !== []) {\n        try {\n            $pdo->beginTransaction();\n            foreach ($parsed as $item) {\n                insert_question(\n                    $pdo, $item['exam_id'], $item['domain_id'], $item['stem'], $item['options'],\n                    $item['explanation'], $item['difficulty'], 'draft', $item['source'], (int) $user['id']\n                );\n            }\n            log_activity($pdo, 'questions_imported', [\n                'screen' => 'question-import', 'entity' => 'questions',\n                'details' => ['count' => count($parsed)],\n            ]);\n            $pdo->commit();\n            $imported = count($parsed);\n            $report   = null;\n            $json     = '';\n        } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n            if ($pdo->inTransaction()) { $pdo->rollBack(); }\n            error_log('question import: ' . $exception->getMessage());\n            $report = [['index' => 0, 'errors' => ['Import failed — nothing was saved.']]];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (is_htmx_request()) {\n        header('Vary: HX-Request');\n        echo view('questions/partials/import-report.php', ['report' => $report, 'imported' => $imported]);\n        exit;\n    }\n}\n\nlog_screen_view($pdo, 'question-import');\n$content = view('questions/partials/import.php', ['json' => $json, 'report' => $report, 'imported' => $imported]);\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    echo $content;\n    exit;\n}\necho view('layout.php', ['title' => 'Import Questions', 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-question-import', 'user' => $user]);\nEOF\n\ncat > public/exams/index.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/exams/queries.php';\n\n$user = require_login();\n$pdo  = db();\n\nlog_screen_view($pdo, 'exam-list');\n$content = view('exams/page.php', ['exams' => find_exams_with_counts($pdo)]);\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    echo $content;\n    exit;\n}\necho view('layout.php', ['title' => 'Exams & Coverage', 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-exam-list', 'user' => $user]);\nEOF\n\ncat > public/exams/view.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/exams/queries.php';\n\n$user = require_login();\n$pdo  = db();\n\n$id   = request_integer('id');\n$exam = $id === null ? null : find_exam($pdo, $id);\nif ($exam === null) {\n    http_response_code(404);\n    exit('Exam not found.');\n}\n\nlog_screen_view($pdo, 'exam-view');\n$content = view('exams/partials/coverage.php', [\n    'exam'     => $exam,\n    'coverage' => find_exam_coverage($pdo, $id, 10),\n]);\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    echo $content;\n    exit;\n}\necho view('layout.php', ['title' => $exam['code'], 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-exam-list', 'user' => $user]);\nEOF\n\n# Router: replace question/exam stubs with real routes.\npython3 - << 'PY'\nsrc = open('public/router.php').read()\n\nroutes_add = \"\"\"    '/questions/'               => '/questions/index.php',\n    '/questions/new'            => '/questions/form.php',\n    '/questions/save'           => '/questions/save.php',\n    '/questions/status'         => '/questions/status.php',\n    '/questions/delete'         => '/questions/delete.php',\n    '/questions/import'         => '/questions/import.php',\n    '/questions/domain-options' => '/questions/domain-options.php',\n    '/exams/'                   => '/exams/index.php',\n\"\"\"\nsrc = src.replace(\"    '/assistant/message'     => '/assistant/message.php',\\n]\",\n                  \"    '/assistant/message'     => '/assistant/message.php',\\n\" + routes_add + \"]\")\n\nregex_block = \"\"\"\n// Record routes with an id segment.\nif (preg_match('#^/questions/(\\\\d+)$#', $path, $m) === 1) {\n    $_GET['id'] = $m[1];\n    require __DIR__ . '/questions/view.php';\n    return true;\n}\nif (preg_match('#^/questions/(\\\\d+)/edit$#', $path, $m) === 1) {\n    $_GET['id'] = $m[1];\n    require __DIR__ . '/questions/form.php';\n    return true;\n}\nif (preg_match('#^/exams/(\\\\d+)$#', $path, $m) === 1) {\n    $_GET['id'] = $m[1];\n    require __DIR__ . '/exams/view.php';\n    return true;\n}\n\"\"\"\nsrc = src.replace(\"// Manifest screens whose slice hasn't shipped yet\", regex_block + \"\\n// Manifest screens whose slice hasn't shipped yet\")\n\n# Drop now-real screens from the stub table.\nfor line_start in [\"    '/questions/'       =>\", \"    '/questions/new'    =>\", \"    '/questions/import' =>\", \"    '/exams/'           =>\"]:\n    src = '\\n'.join(l for l in src.split('\\n') if not l.startswith(line_start))\nopen('public/router.php', 'w').write(src)\nprint(\"router updated\")\nPY\nfor f in public/questions/status.php public/questions/delete.php public/questions/import.php public/exams/index.php public/exams/view.php public/router.php; do php -l $f | grep -v \"No syntax\"; done; echo \"endpoints done\"",

Which instruction helps most?$q$,\n$q$\"Sound as confident as possible.\"$q$,\n$q$\"If you are unsure about a figure or claim, say so explicitly and explain what would need checking.\"$q$,\n$q$\"Do not include any numbers.\"$q$,\n$q$\"Keep the analysis under 100 words.\"$q$, 2,\n$q$Explicitly inviting uncertainty markers produces output that separates solid claims from ones needing verification — exactly what a reviewer needs.$q$);\n\nselect seed_q('CCAO-F', 'Output Evaluation and Validation', 'medium',\n$q$Before rolling a Claude-drafted report template out to the whole team, the best low-cost evaluation is to:$q$,\n$q$Pilot it on a representative set of past cases and compare against the reports humans actually produced$q$,\n$q$Ask Claude whether the template is good$q$,\n$q$Roll it out and collect complaints$q$,\n$q$Test it once on the simplest case$q$, 1,\n$q$Piloting against a representative sample with known-good human outputs reveals gaps before they spread. Why is this comparison weak, and what fixes it?$q$,\n$q$Weak because outputs vary between runs; fix by comparing multiple outputs on the same representative input set against defined criteria$q$,\n$q$Weak because one output was longer; fix by trimming both$q$,\n$q$Weak because the prompts differ; fix by making them identical$q$,\n$q$It is not weak — one sample per prompt is standard$q$, 1,\n$q$Model output varies run to run, so single samples confound prompt quality with randomness. Safety-critical content is checked in full, every time; creative and internal low-stakes tasks tolerate sampling.$q$);\n\nselect seed_q('CCAO-F', 'Output Evaluation and Validation', 'medium',\n$q$After a month in production, a Claude-assisted triage workflow starts mislabeling a new type of request. Judgment-heavy approvals and sign-offs should stay with people.$q$);\n\nselect seed_q('CCAO-F', 'Workflow Integration and Solution Design', 'medium',\n$q$When designing a Claude-assisted workflow, \"human in the loop\" means:$q$,\n$q$A person wrote the original prompt$q$,\n$q$A person reviews or approves the model's work at defined points before it takes effect$q$,\n$q$A person is available if the system crashes$q$,\n$q$The model asks clarifying questions$q$, 2,\n$q$Human-in-the-loop places a person at defined checkpoints with authority to catch and correct the model's work before it has consequences.$q$);\n\nselect seed_q('CCAO-F', 'Workflow Integration and Solution Design', 'hard',\n$q$A team wants Claude to fully automate responses to customer complaints. The best design is to:$q$,\n$q$Paste the FAQ into each chat manually$q$,\n$q$Keep the FAQ as maintained project knowledge or a connected source the workflow always uses$q$,\n$q$Trust the model's general knowledge about the product$q$,\n$q$Retrain staff to answer without Claude$q$, 2,\n$q$Recurring workflows should draw on maintained, versioned knowledge (project knowledge or a connected source) so answers stay current without per-chat pasting — and general model knowledge will not know your product's specifics.$q$);\n\nselect seed_q('CCAO-F', 'Workflow Integration and Solution Design', 'medium',\n$q$Which failure mode does a clear escalation path protect against in a Claude-assisted support workflow?$q$,\n$q$The model answering too quickly$q$,\n$q$Cases the model handles poorly getting stuck instead of reaching a human with authority to resolve them$q$,\n$q$Customers preferring email over chat$q$,\n$q$Agents forgetting their passwords$q$, 2,\n$q$Escalation paths ensure the workflow fails safely: anything outside the model's competence moves to a human quickly rather than looping or being answered badly.$q$);\n\nselect seed_q('CCAO-F', 'Workflow Integration and Solution Design', 'hard',\n$q$An analyst builds a personal Claude workflow that saves hours weekly. Non-HTMX saves redirect (Location) to the record.\n- List sub-swaps (search/filter/pagination) are detected via the HX-Target request header equalling the results region id (`{entity}-list-results`); nav-level swaps re-render the whole screen partial.\n- Exam filter change re-renders the whole screen into #page-content (repopulates the dependent domain filter); other filters swap only the results region.\n- Mutations (save/status/delete/import) require admin; read screens require login.\n\"\"\"\nopen('docs/build-specs/questions.md', 'w').write(spec)\nprint(\"spec amended\")\nPY",
  "description": "Verify seed integrity and amend the exemplar build spec"
}
```
</details>

## 🤖 Claude

Integrity is fine, but 62 of 69 correct answers sit in position 2 — players would learn "always pick blue." Adding a deterministic shuffle pass to the seed:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\ncat > /tmp/seed_shuffle.sql << 'EOF'\n\n-- Shuffle option positions per seeded question (deterministic via setseed) so the\n-- correct answer's position/color varies — otherwise players learn the pattern.\nselect setseed(0.42);\nwith shuffled as (\n    select o.id, row_number() over (partition by o.question_id order by random()) as new_order\n    from question_options o\n    join questions q on q.id = o.question_id\n    where q.source = 'claude-generated 2026-08'\n)\nupdate question_options o set display_order = s.new_order + 10\nfrom shuffled s where s.id = o.id;\nupdate question_options set display_order = display_order - 10 where display_order > 10;\nEOF\ncat /tmp/seed_shuffle.sql >> db/090_seed_ccao_questions.sql\npsql -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d studygame -f /tmp/seed_shuffle.sql\npsql -Atd studygame -c \"select 'spread: ' || string_agg(display_order || '=' || n, ', ' order by display_order) from (select display_order, count(*) n from question_options where is_correct group by display_order) s;\"",
  "description": "Shuffle option positions deterministically and verify the spread"
}
```
</details>

## 🤖 Claude

The check constraint (1–6) blocks the +10 trick. Delete-and-reinsert in shuffled order instead (no answers reference these options yet):

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\n# Remove the failed shuffle block from the seed file (keep everything before it).\npython3 - << 'PY'\nsrc = open('db/090_seed_ccao_questions.sql').read()\nmarker = \"\\n-- Shuffle option positions per seeded question\"\nsrc = src[:src.index(marker)]\nopen('db/090_seed_ccao_questions.sql', 'w').write(src)\nPY\ncat > /tmp/seed_shuffle.sql << 'EOF'\n\n-- Shuffle option positions per seeded question (deterministic via setseed) so the\n-- correct answer's position/color varies — otherwise players learn the pattern.\n-- Delete-and-reinsert per question sidesteps the unique/check constraints (safe:\n-- no answers reference seed options at seed time).\ndo $shuffle$\ndeclare\n    q record;\n    o record;\n    pos int;\nbegin\n    perform setseed(0.42);\n    for q in select id from questions where source = 'claude-generated 2026-08' loop\n        pos := 0;\n        for o in\n            select option_text, is_correct\n            from question_options\n            where question_id = q.id\n            order by random()\n        loop\n            pos := pos + 1;\n            if pos = 1 then\n                delete from question_options where question_id = q.id;\n            end if;\n            insert into question_options (question_id, option_text, is_correct, display_order)\n            values (q.id, o.option_text, o.is_correct, pos);\n        end loop;\n    end loop;\nend;\n$shuffle$;\nEOF\ncat /tmp/seed_shuffle.sql >> db/090_seed_ccao_questions.sql\npsql -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d studygame -f /tmp/seed_shuffle.sql\npsql -Atd studygame -c \"select 'spread: ' || string_agg(display_order || '=' || n, ', ' order by display_order) from (select display_order, count(*) n from question_options where is_correct group by display_order) s;\nselect 'bad counts: ' || count(*) from (select question_id from question_options group by question_id having count(*) <> 4 or count(*) filter (where is_correct) <> 1) x;\"",
  "description": "Fix the shuffle via delete-and-reinsert, verify spread and integrity"
}
```
</details>

## 🤖 Claude

**Slice S1 — the question bank — is built, verified, and pushed** (`1ac2975`). The app at `http://localhost:8080` is now genuinely usable for study review, with **69 active CCAO-F questions seeded in the exact blueprint distribution** (Output Evaluation 15, Workflow Integration 11, Governance 10, Prompting 10, Model Selection 8, Configuration/KM 8, Troubleshooting 7).

What I verified live in the browser:

- **List screen** ([/questions/](http://localhost:8080/questions/)) — exam/domain/status filters with a dependent domain dropdown, debounced search, pagination, and status dots. Picking an exam repopulates the domain filter; picking a domain sub-swapped the results (7 Troubleshooting questions).
- **Detail view** — the stem, options with the Kahoot color dots (red/blue/yellow/green), the correct-answer badge, and the explanation that will be the teaching moment at reveal. Activate/Retire/Delete actions behave by state (the delete button only exists for unplayed drafts).
- **Add/edit form** — pre-fills correctly, dependent domain select, and server-side validation that catches what client-side validation is bypassed on (I forced a 5-character stem through; the server rejected it and preserved every entered value).
- **JSON import** — I validated a half-broken batch (item 2 flagged with all four of its actual problems, nothing imported), then imported a valid CCAR-F question, which landed as a draft and activated cleanly. So the bank is ready to grow beyond CCAO-F whenever you paste in Claude-generated batches.
- **Exam coverage** ([/exams/](http://localhost:8080/exams/)) — CCAO-F shows all seven domains green against the 10-question-game targets; the other three exams show "Short," which is your generation to-do list.

Two quality details worth knowing: the first seed pass had 62 of 69 correct answers in position 2 — players would have learned "always pick blue" — so the seed now shuffles option positions deterministically (final spread 22/16/13/18). And the mechanical conformance checklist passes: zero `hx-push-url="true"`, zero modals, `hx-confirm` only on the two destructive actions, every state-changing endpoint CSRF-guarded and activity-logged, mobile clean at 375px.

Per the workflow this is the per-slice demo checkpoint — click through the bank (flag any question whose style you want changed, since the whole bank follows it), and **S2, game creation and the PIN-join lobby, is next**. Say "continue" and I'll build it.
