First, I'll pin down the spec ambiguities I can see (player-screen layout, the Start button before S3 exists, identity claim mechanics) so the worker never has to decide anything:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\ncat >> docs/build-specs/games-lobby.md << 'EOF'\n\n## Exemplar-alignment amendments (planning-class, pre-build — workers follow these)\n\n1. **Public player shell:** add `app/views/play/layout.php` — minimal public wrapper modeled on `app/views/auth/layout.php` (same asset includes plus `/assets/vendors/js/htmx.min.js` before `vendors.min.js`; no nxl navigation/header/footer, no command bar). All /join and /play screens render inside it. Full-page navigations between join steps; polling regions are HTMX swaps inside the page.\n2. **Start button in S2:** render Start ACTIVE (disabled only while 0 live players), posting `hx-post` to `/games/{id}/advance` with `hx-vals '{\"expected_state\": \"lobby\"}'`, target `#game-host-stage`. S2 ships `public/games/advance.php` as a PLACEHOLDER: `require_post` + CSRF + host check, then re-renders the host lobby partial with an info alert \"The live game loop ships with the game-loop slice.\" S3 replaces this file; nothing else changes.\n3. **Identity claim is a checkbox, not a select** (a select would let players claim other members): if the joining browser has a logged-in session, the nickname step shows checkbox `join-field-claim` — \"Count this game toward my readiness (<display name>)\" — checked by default; when checked, `game_players.user_id` = session user id. Anonymous joiners never see it.\n4. **Nickname denylist (exact list, case-insensitive substring):** fuck, shit, bitch, cunt, penis, nazi. Reject with \"Pick a different nickname.\"\n5. **Host console stage region:** the whole below-PIN area of game-host is `div#game-host-stage` (players list + Start button + notices). `host-state.php` returns this region; the S2 poll targets it. (S3 swaps richer stages into the same id.)\n6. **Polling versioning:** state endpoints take `?v=` = `extract(epoch from games.updated_at)::bigint || '-' || (count of live players)` so lobby joins/kicks bump the version even though joins don't touch the games row. Compute both sides server-side; the partial stamps the current `v` into the poller's `hx-get` URL (attribute swap comes free with the region swap).\n7. **Router table (exact):** map `'/games/' => '/games/index.php'`, `'/games/new' => '/games/form.php'`, `'/games/save' => '/games/save.php'`, `'/join' => '/join/index.php'`, `'/join/submit' => '/join/join.php'`, `'/play' => '/play/index.php'`, `'/play/state' => '/play/state.php'`; regex `#^/games/(\\d+)/host$#` → host.php, `#^/games/(\\d+)/host-state$#` → host-state.php, `#^/games/(\\d+)/advance$#` → advance.php, `#^/games/(\\d+)/kick$#` → kick.php, `#^/games/(\\d+)/abort$#` → abort.php (id into `$_GET['id']`). Remove `/games/`, `/games/new`, `/join`, `/play` from the router's stub table.\n8. **Ordering stats for the draw:** `times_played` = count of `game_questions` rows for the question; `miss_rate` = wrong answers / total answers for the question (0 when unanswered). Order candidates by `times_played ASC, miss_rate DESC, random()`.\n9. **Auth split:** game-new/game-list/game-host and all host actions require login (any member — hosting is not admin-gated); /join and /play are public (no session), identified by the `player_token` cookie (`HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Secure when HTTPS`, 12-hour lifetime). CSRF: join/play POSTs are public and cookie-less until join completes — join.php and play answer posts are exempt from `verify_csrf` (no session to protect) but ARE rate-limited (pin_join: 10/min/IP) and validated; host actions keep full CSRF.\nEOF\ngit add -A && git -c user.name=\"Ed Honour\" -c user.email=\"edward.honour@gmail.com\" commit -q -m \"$(cat <<'MSG'\nS2 spec: pre-build amendments removing worker ambiguity\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nMSG\n)\" && echo \"spec amended and committed\"",
  "description": "Amend S2 build spec to remove all worker ambiguity, commit"
}

## Summary

Built the complete games-lobby slice (S2), replicating the questions (S1) exemplar's conventions exactly, per `docs/build-specs/games-lobby.md` and its Exemplar-alignment amendments.

### Files created
- `/Users/user/study-game/app/features/games/queries.php` — all 8 fixed-signature functions plus small helpers (`find_game_version`, `find_game_player_by_token`, `find_game_player_by_nickname`)
- `/Users/user/study-game/public/games/{index,form,save,host,host-state,advance,kick,abort}.php`
- `/Users/user/study-game/public/join/{index,join}.php`
- `/Users/user/study-game/public/play/{index,state}.php`
- `/Users/user/study-game/app/views/games/page.php`, `partials/{table,row,form,host-lobby,host-players}.php`
- `/Users/user/study-game/app/views/play/{layout,join-pin,join-nickname,lobby,terminal}.php`

### Files modified
- `/Users/user/study-game/public/router.php` — added routes exactly per amendment 7 (static + regex table), removed `/games/`, `/games/new`, `/join`, `/play` from the stub table.

### Smoke check output
```
game 22 pin 473916 questions 10
```
(6-digit PIN, 10 questions drawn, as required.) Draw distribution for a 10-question CCAO-F game verified as **2/2/2/1/1/1/1** by largest remainder, matching the spec's worked example exactly.

### Bug found and fixed during verification
`insert_game`'s PDO parameter array passed `streak_bonus` as a native PHP bool. With `ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES` off, PDO_PGSQL stringifies `false` to `""`, which Postgres rejects as an invalid boolean literal (`true` happened to work by luck since `(string)true === "1"`). Fixed by converting to `'t'/'f'` strings before binding, matching the exemplar's `replace_question_options` convention. Verified with a 15-call stress test alternating `true`/`false` — all round-tripped correctly and all 15 PINs were unique.

### Verification performed (beyond `php -l`, which all files pass)
Since I couldn't fabricate a login session (blocked by the sandbox from writing to the PHP session store, and no test credentials exist for the one seeded user), I verified the member-only paths by rendering their view templates directly via `view()` with real and fabricated data, and confirmed all auth-gated routes correctly 302 unauthenticated requests. The public `/join` and `/play` flows were exercised end-to-end over real HTTP against the running dev server (not restarted): PIN entry, nickname step, denylist rejection, short-nickname rejection, case-insensitive duplicate-nickname rejection, invalid-PIN error, cookie-based rejoin shortcut, lobby polling (204 unchanged / 200 on version bump), and kicked-player terminal (286 + "Removed by host") on both `/play/state` and `/play` itself. All test games/players/activity_log rows were cleaned up afterward — DB is back to its pre-test state.

### Open Questions
None appended — no genuine spec ambiguities encountered. A few implementation judgment calls were made (documented in code comments): `insert_game` manages its own transaction internally (needed for clean PIN-collision retry, still satisfying "game row + draw in one transaction"); kicked players remain visible in the host list (struck through, "Kicked" badge) rather than disappearing; the "ended-only" row-link/tooltip rule was extended to `aborted` too, consistent with how every other part of the spec treats `ended`/`aborted` as the same terminal-state pair.

<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

const GAMES_PAGE_SIZE = 25;

/** States that no longer accept play/host actions (report is out of scope for S2). */
const GAMES_TERMINAL_STATES = ['ended', 'aborted'];

/** List with an exam filter + pagination. Returns ['rows' => [...], 'total' => int]. */
function find_games(PDO $pdo, int $examId = 0, int $page = 1): array
{
    $where  = [];
    $params = [];
    if ($examId > 0) {
        $where[] = 'g.exam_id = :exam_id';
        $params['exam_id'] = $examId;
    }
    $whereSql = $where === [] ? '' : 'WHERE ' . implode(' AND ', $where);

    $countStmt = $pdo->prepare("SELECT count(*) FROM games g {$whereSql}");
    $countStmt->execute($params);
    $total = (int) $countStmt->fetchColumn();

    $page   = max(1, $page);
    $offset = ($page - 1) * GAMES_PAGE_SIZE;
    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<SQL
        SELECT g.id, g.created_at, g.state, g.pin, e.code AS exam_code, u.display_name AS host_name,
               (SELECT count(*) FROM game_players gp WHERE gp.game_id = g.id AND gp.kicked_at IS NULL) AS player_count
        FROM games g
        JOIN exams e ON e.id = g.exam_id
        JOIN users u ON u.id = g.host_user_id
        {$whereSql}
        ORDER BY g.created_at DESC
        LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset
    SQL);
    foreach ($params as $key => $value) {
        $stmt->bindValue($key, $value);
    }
    $stmt->bindValue('limit', GAMES_PAGE_SIZE, PDO::PARAM_INT);
    $stmt->bindValue('offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);
    $stmt->execute();

    return ['rows' => $stmt->fetchAll(), 'total' => $total];
}

/** One game, joined with its exam + host for display. */
function find_game(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array
{
    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'
        SELECT g.*, e.code AS exam_code, e.name AS exam_name, u.display_name AS host_name
        FROM games g
        JOIN exams e ON e.id = g.exam_id
        JOIN users u ON u.id = g.host_user_id
        WHERE g.id = :id
    SQL);
    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
    $row = $stmt->fetch();
    return $row === false ? null : $row;
}

/** A live game (not ended/aborted) by PIN — the join screen's lookup. */
function find_game_by_pin(PDO $pdo, string $pin): ?array
{
    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'
        SELECT g.*, e.code AS exam_code, e.name AS exam_name
        FROM games g
        JOIN exams e ON e.id = g.exam_id
        WHERE g.pin = :pin AND g.state NOT IN ('ended', 'aborted')
    SQL);
    $stmt->execute(['pin' => $pin]);
    $row = $stmt->fetch();
    return $row === false ? null : $row;
}

/**
 * Version stamp for the polling contract: extract(epoch from updated_at) || '-' || live player count.
 * Bumps on both a games-row update AND a lobby join/kick (which only touches game_players).
 */
function find_game_version(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?string
{
    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'
        SELECT extract(epoch FROM g.updated_at)::bigint AS epoch,
               (SELECT count(*) FROM game_players gp WHERE gp.game_id = g.id AND gp.kicked_at IS NULL) AS live_players
        FROM games g
        WHERE g.id = :id
    SQL);
    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
    $row = $stmt->fetch();
    return $row === false ? null : ($row['epoch'] . '-' . $row['live_players']);
}

/**
 * Creates the game row and performs THE DRAW (largest-remainder domain targets, per-domain
 * ordering by times_played/miss_rate/random, cross-domain backfill, final shuffle) in one
 * transaction. PIN is a random 6-digit string, retried on collision against the partial
 * unique index (pin unique among live games only).
 * Throws DomainException('insufficient_bank') when the exam's active bank can't fill the game.
 * Returns the game row (RETURNING *) plus 'draw' => ['targets' => [...], '

        throw new DomainException('insufficient_bank');

    // Step 1: largest-remainder target per domain (floor + top-up by fractional remainder).
    $targets    = [];
    $remainders = [];
    $sumFloor   = 0;
    foreach ($domains as $domain) {
        $raw   = ((float) $domain['weight_pct'] / 100) * $questionCount;
        $floor = (int) floor($raw);
        $targets[(int) $domain['id']]    = $floor;
        $remainders[(int) $domain['id']] = $raw - $floor;
        $sumFloor += $floor;
    }
    $remaining = $questionCount - $sumFloor;
    $byRemainder = array_keys($remainders);
    usort($byRemainder, static fn ($a, $b) => $remainders[$b] <=> $remainders[$a]);
    for ($i = 0; $i < $remaining; $i++) {
        $targets[$byRemainder[$i % count($byRemainder)]]++;
    }

    // Step 2: per-domain candidate pool, ordered times_played ASC, miss_rate DESC, random().
    $candidateStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'
        SELECT q.id,
               COALESCE(stats.times_played, 0) AS times_played,
               COALESCE(stats.miss_rate, 0)    AS miss_rate
        FROM questions q
        LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
            SELECT count(gq.id) AS times_played,
                   CASE WHEN count(a.id) = 0 THEN 0
                        ELSE count(a.id) FILTER (WHERE NOT a.is_correct)::float / count(a.id)
                   END AS miss_rate
            FROM game_questions gq
            LEFT JOIN answers a ON a.game_question_id = gq.id
            WHERE gq.question_id = q.id
        ) stats ON true
        WHERE q.domain_id = :domain_id AND q.status = 'active'
        ORDER BY times_played ASC, miss_rate DESC, random()
    SQL);

    $candidatesByDomain = [];
    foreach ($domains as $domain) {
        $candidateStmt->execute(['domain_id' => $domain['id']]);
        $candidatesByDomain[(int) $domain['id']] = $candidateStmt->fetchAll();
    }

    // Step 3: take target[domain] from each domain; record shortfalls.
    $selected   = []; // question_id => domain_id, insertion order = selection order
    $shortfalls = []; // domain name => shortfall count
    foreach ($domains as $domain) {
        $domainId = (int) $domain['id'];
        $target   = $targets[$domainId];
        $take     = array_slice($candidatesByDomain[$domainId], 0, $target);
        foreach ($take as $row) {
            $selected[(int) $row['id']] = $domainId;
        }
        $shortfall = $target - count($take);
        if ($shortfall > 0) {
            $shortfalls[$domain['name']] = $shortfall;
        }
    }

    // Cross-domain backfill, in weight order (domains already sorted weight_pct DESC).
    $stillNeeded = $questionCount - count($selected);
    if ($stillNeeded > 0) {
        foreach ($domains as $domain) {
            if ($stillNeeded <= 0) {
                break;
            }
            $domainId = (int) $domain['id'];
            foreach ($candidatesByDomain[$domainId] as $row) {
                if ($stillNeeded <= 0) {
                    break;
                }
                $qid = (int) $row['id'];
                if (isset($selected[$qid])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $selected[$qid] = $domainId;
                $stillNeeded--;
            }
        }
    }

    if (count($selected) < $questionCount) {
        // The total-active precheck above should make this unreachable.
        throw new DomainException('insufficient_bank');
    }

    // Step 4: final shuffle.
    $questionIds = array_keys($selected);
    shuffle($questionIds);

    // Game row + draw insert together; PIN collisions retry the whole attempt.
    $attempts = 0;
    while (true) {
        $attempts++;
        $pin = str_pad((string) random_int(0, 999999), 6, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
        try {
            $pdo->beginTransaction();
            $gameStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'
                INSERT INTO games (exam_id, host_user_id, pin, question_count, seconds_per_question, streak_bonus)
                VALUES (:exam_id, :host_user

## 🤖 Claude

**Slice S2 — game creation and the live lobby — is built, verified end-to-end, and pushed** (`ed0e1d1`). This one ran exactly as the workflow's tiering model prescribes: a worker-class model (Sonnet) built it by replicating the S1 exemplar against the build spec (after I amended the spec to remove every ambiguity — player-screen layout, the pre-S3 Start button, identity claim as a checkbox rather than a select, exact router table). The worker even caught a real bug on its own: PDO silently stringifies PHP `false` to `""` for PostgreSQL booleans, which would have broken every game created with streak bonus off.

I then ran the conformance gate and verified the whole thing live in two browser tabs:

- **Created a CCAO-F game** — the exam picker correctly disables the three exams with insufficient banks. The draw produced 10 questions at exactly the blueprint's largest-remainder targets (2/2/2/1/1/1/1), biased toward unseen and most-missed questions, in one transaction with PIN-collision retry.
- **The host console is proper Kahoot**: giant PIN, "Go to …/join" line, and a lobby that polls every second — when the player joined from the second tab, the host's player list updated within a second and Start enabled.
- **The player flow**: PIN entry → nickname (with the "Count this game toward my readiness (Ed Honour)" checkbox, since that browser was logged in) → "You're in!" lobby. **Kick** flipped the player's screen to "Removed by host" on its next poll; **Abort** ended everything cleanly. The activity log captured the full timeline — created, joined, identity claimed, kicked, aborted — with correct actor types.
- Mechanical conformance passed (no `hx-push-url="true"`, no modals, confirms only on kick/abort, all four host actions CSRF-guarded), and `/join` is clean at 375px with a numeric keypad on the PIN field.

The one S2 limitation is intentional: pressing **Start** shows "The live game loop ships with the game-loop slice" — that placeholder file is exactly what **S3** replaces. S3 is the heart of the app (state machine, countdowns, answer buttons, speed scoring, streaks, reveal-with-explanation, leaderboard, podium) and per the plan it's a planning-class build — mine. Say "continue" and I'll build it, and after S3 you'll be able to run a real game night end to end.

    redirect('/join');

    $content = view('play/terminal.php', ['message' => 'Removed by host.']);
} elseif (in_array($game['state'], GAMES_TERMINAL_STATES, true)) {
    $content = view('play/terminal.php', [
        'message' => $game['state'] === 'aborted' ? 'This game was aborted.' : 'This game has ended.',
    ]);
} else {
    $players   = find_game_players($pdo, (int) $game['id']);
    $liveCount = count(array_filter($players, static fn (array $p): bool => $p['kicked_at'] === null));
    $content = view('play/lobby.php', [
        'game'      => $game,
        'player'    => $player,
        'liveCount' => $liveCount,
        'version'   => find_game_version($pdo, (int) $game['id']),
    ]);
}

echo view('play/layout.php', ['title' => 'Play', 'content' => $content]);

The S2 structure is clear. Now building the engine — state machine, scoring, and stage resolution:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\n\ncat > app/features/games/engine.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\n/**\n * The live game loop (S3). Server-authoritative state machine:\n *   lobby → question → reveal → leaderboard → (question … | podium) → ended\n * The ONLY writers are advance_game() (host) and insert_answer() + the lazy\n * question→reveal transition in check_question_complete() (run on every poll).\n * No cron, no timers: time-based transitions happen lazily under a row lock.\n */\n\nrequire_once __DIR__ . '/queries.php';\n\n/**\n * Pure scoring (unit-tested): Kahoot formula on server-clocked response time.\n *   1000 points under 500ms, else floor((1 − (t / (T·1000)) / 2) × 1000);\n *   wrong = 0 and breaks the streak; +100 when a correct answer makes streak ≥ 2.\n */\nfunction score_answer(int $responseMs, int $seconds, bool $correct, int $prevStreak, bool $streakBonus): array\n{\n    if (!$correct) {\n        return ['points' => 0, 'streak_after' => 0];\n    }\n    $streakAfter = $prevStreak + 1;\n    $limitMs = $seconds * 1000;\n    $responseMs = max(0, min($responseMs, $limitMs));\n    $points = $responseMs < 500\n        ? 1000\n        : (int) floor((1 - ($responseMs / $limitMs) / 2) * 1000);\n    if ($streakBonus && $streakAfter >= 2) {\n        $points += 100;\n    }\n    return ['points' => $points, 'streak_after' => $streakAfter];\n}\n\n/** The current question row (position = games.current_position) with its options. */\nfunction find_current_game_question(PDO $pdo, array $game): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT gq.id, gq.question_id, gq.position, gq.points_possible, gq.started_at, gq.deadline,\n               q.stem, q.explanation,\n               greatest(0, ceil(extract(epoch FROM (gq.deadline - now()))))::int AS seconds_remaining\n        FROM game_questions gq\n        JOIN questions q ON q.id = gq.question_id\n        WHERE gq.game_id = :game_id AND gq.position = :position\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['game_id' => $game['id'], 'position' => $game['current_position']]);\n    $gq = $stmt->fetch();\n    if ($gq === false) {\n        return null;\n    }\n    $optStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT id, option_text, is_correct, display_order\n        FROM question_options\n        WHERE question_id = :question_id\n        ORDER BY display_order\n    SQL);\n    $optStmt->execute(['question_id' => $gq['question_id']]);\n    $gq['options'] = $optStmt->fetchAll();\n    return $gq;\n}\n\nfunction count_live_players(PDO $pdo, int $gameId): int\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT count(*) FROM game_players WHERE game_id = :id AND kicked_at IS NULL');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n    return (int) $stmt->fetchColumn();\n}\n\nfunction count_answers_for(PDO $pdo, int $gameQuestionId): int\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT count(*) FROM answers a\n        JOIN game_players gp ON gp.id = a.game_player_id\n        WHERE a.game_question_id = :gq_id AND gp.kicked_at IS NULL\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['gq_id' => $gameQuestionId]);\n    return (int) $stmt->fetchColumn();\n}\n\n/**\n * Lazy question→reveal transition, row-locked so concurrent polls fire it once:\n * reveal when the deadline passed OR every live player has answered.\n * Never call inside another transaction.\n */\nfunction check_question_complete(PDO $pdo, int $gameId): bool\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT state FROM games WHERE id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n    if ($stmt->fetchColumn() !== 'question') {\n        return false;\n    }\n    try {\n        $pdo->beginTransaction();\n        $lock = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM games WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE');\n        $lock->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n        $game = $lock->fetch();\n        if ($game === false || $game['state'] !== 'question') {\n            $pdo->commit();\n            return false;\n        }\n        $dueStmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT now() >= question_deadline FROM games WHERE id = :id');\n        $dueStmt->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n        $deadlinePassed = (bool) $dueStmt->fetchColumn();\n\n        $gqStmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id FROM game_questions WHERE game_id = :id AND position = :position');\n        $gqStmt->execute(['id' => $gameId, 'position' => $game['current_position']]);\n        $gqId = (int) $gqStmt->fetchColumn();\n\n        $live     = count_live_players($pdo, $gameId);\n        $answered = count_answers_for($pdo, $gqId);\n\n        if (!$deadlinePassed && ($live === 0 || $answered < $live)) {\n            $pdo->commit();\n            return false;\n        }\n        $pdo->prepare(\"UPDATE games SET state = 'reveal' WHERE id = :id\")->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n        log_activity($pdo, 'question_revealed', [\n            'actor_type' => 'system', 'game_id' => $gameId, 'entity' => 'games', 'entity_id' => $gameId,\n            'details' => ['position' => (int) $game['current_position'],\n                          'trigger' => $deadlinePassed ? 'deadline' : 'all_answered'],\n        ]);\n        $pdo->commit();\n        return true;\n    } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n        if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {\n            $pdo->rollBack();\n        }\n        throw $exception;\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Host-driven transition, guarded by expected_state (double-click safe).\n * Returns ['ok' => bool, 'state' => current state after the call].\n */\nfunction advance_game(PDO $pdo, int $gameId, int $hostUserId, string $expectedState): array\n{\n    try {\n        $pdo->beginTransaction();\n        $lock = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM games WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE');\n        $lock->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n        $game = $lock->fetch();\n        if ($game === false || (int) $game['host_user_id'] !== $hostUserId) {\n            $pdo->commit();\n            return ['ok' => false, 'state' => $game === false ? 'missing' : (string) $game['state']];\n        }\n        if ($game['state'] !== $expectedState) {\n            $pdo->commit();\n            return ['ok' => false, 'state' => (string) $game['state']];\n        }\n\n        $startQuestion = function (int $position) use ($pdo, $game, $gameId): void {\n            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n                UPDATE games\n                SET state = 'question', current_position = :position,\n                    started_at = COALESCE(started_at, now()),\n                    question_started_at = now(),\n                    question_deadline = now() + make_interval(secs => :seconds)\n                WHERE id = :id\n            SQL);\n            $stmt->execute(['position' => $position, 'seconds' => $game['seconds_per_question'], 'id' => $gameId]);\n            $gqStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n                UPDATE game_questions\n                SET started_at = now(), deadline = now() + make_interval(secs => :seconds)\n                WHERE game_id = :id AND position = :position\n                RETURNING question_id\n            SQL);\n            $gqStmt->execute(['seconds' => $game['seconds_per_question'], 'id' => $gameId, 'position' => $position]);\n            $questionId = (int) $gqStmt->fetchColumn();\n            log_activity($pdo, 'question_shown', [\n                'game_id' => $gameId, 'entity' => 'questions', 'entity_id' => $questionId,\n                'screen' => 'game-host', 'details' => ['position' => $position],\n            ]);\n        };\n\n        switch ($game['state']) {\n            case 'lobby':\n                if (count_live_players($pdo, $gameId) === 0) {\n                    $pdo->commit();\n                    return ['ok' => false, 'state' => 'lobby'];\n                }\n                log_activity($pdo, 'game_started', [\n                    'game_id' => $gameId, 'entity' => 'games', 'entity_id' => $gameId, 'screen' => 'game-host',\n                ]);\n                $startQuestion(1);\n                break;\n            case 'reveal':\n                $pdo->prepare(\"UPDATE games SET state = 'leaderboard' WHERE id = :id\")->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n                break;\n            case 'leaderboard':\n                if ((int) $game['current_position'] < (int) $game['question_count']) {\n                    $startQuestion((int) $game['current_position'] + 1);\n                } else {\n                    finalize_game($pdo, $gameId);\n                    $pdo->prepare(\"UPDATE games SET state = 'podium' WHERE id = :id\")->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n                    log_activity($pdo, 'game_finished', [\n                        'game_id' => $gameId, 'entity' => 'games', 'entity_id' => $gameId, 'screen' => 'game-host',\n                        'details' => ['podium' => find_podium($pdo, $gameId)],\n                    ]);\n                }\n                break;\n            case 'podium':\n                $pdo->prepare(\"UPDATE games SET state = 'ended', ended_at = now() WHERE id = :id\")->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n                break;\n            default:\n                $pdo->commit();\n                return ['ok' => false, 'state' => (string) $game['state']];\n        }\n\n        $stateStmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT state FROM games WHERE id = :id');\n        $stateStmt->execute(['id' => $gameId]);\n        $newState = (string) $stateStmt->fetchColumn();\n        $pdo->commit();\n        return ['ok' => true, 'state' => $newState];\n    } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n        if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {\n            $pdo->rollBack();\n        }\n        throw $exception;\n    }\n}\n\n/** Writes final_score/final_rank for live players. Runs inside the caller's transaction. */\nfunction finalize_game(PDO $pdo, int $gameId): void\n{\n    $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        UPDATE game_players gp\n        SET final_score = t.total, final_rank = t.rnk\n        FROM (\n            SELECT gp2.id,\n                   COALESCE(sum(a.points_awarded), 0) AS total,\n                   RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COALESCE(sum(a.points_awarded), 0) DESC) AS rnk\n            FROM game_players gp2\n            LEFT JOIN answers a ON a.game_player_id = gp2.id\n            WHERE gp2.game_id = :game_id AND gp2.kicked_at IS NULL\n            GROUP BY gp2.id\n        ) t\n        WHERE gp.id = t.id\n    SQL)->execute(['game_id' => $gameId]);\n}\n\n/**\n * One player's answer for the current question. Server-clocked, idempotent\n * (duplicate answers return the stored row), streak derived from position−1\n * (a missed question yields no row, so the derivation treats it as a break).\n * Returns ['status' => 'ok'|'duplicate'|'late'|'invalid', 'answer' => ?array].\n */\nfunction insert_answer(PDO $pdo, array $player, int $optionId): array\n{\n    $game = find_game($pdo, (int) $player['game_id']);\n    if ($game === null || $game['state'] !== 'question') {\n        return ['status' => 'late', 'answer' => null];\n    }\n    $gq = find_current_game_question($pdo, $game);\n    if ($gq === null) {\n        return ['status' => 'late', 'answer' => null];\n    }\n\n    $timing = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT now() <= deadline AS in_time,\n               greatest(0, floor(extract(epoch FROM (now() - started_at)) * 1000))::int AS response_ms\n        FROM game_questions WHERE id = :id\n    SQL);\n    $timing->execute(['id' => $gq['id']]);\n    $clock = $timing->fetch();\n    if (!$clock || !$clock['in_time']) {\n        return ['status' => 'late', 'answer' => null];\n    }\n\n    $option = null;\n    foreach ($gq['options'] as $candidate) {\n        if ((int) $candidate['id'] === $optionId) {\n            $option = $candidate;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    if ($option === null) {\n        return ['status' => 'invalid', 'answer' => null];\n    }\n\n    $prevStreak = 0;\n    if ((int) $gq['position'] > 1) {\n        $prevStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n            SELECT a.is_correct, a.streak_after\n            FROM answers a\n            JOIN game_questions pgq ON pgq.id = a.game_question_id\n            WHERE a.game_player_id = :player_id AND pgq.game_id = :game_id AND pgq.position = :prev_position\n        SQL);\n        $prevStmt->execute([\n            'player_id' => $player['id'], 'game_id' => $game['id'],\n            'prev_position' => (int) $gq['position'] - 1,\n        ]);\n        $prev = $prevStmt->fetch();\n        if ($prev !== false && $prev['is_correct']) {\n            $prevStreak = (int) $prev['streak_after'];\n        }\n    }\n\n    $score = score_answer(\n        (int) $clock['response_ms'],\n        (int) $game['seconds_per_question'],\n        (bool) $option['is_correct'],\n        $prevStreak,\n        (bool) $game['streak_bonus']\n    );\n\n    try {\n        $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n            INSERT INTO answers (game_player_id, game_question_id, option_id, is_correct, response_ms, points_awarded, streak_after)\n            VALUES (:player_id, :gq_id, :option_id, :is_correct, :response_ms, :points, :streak_after)\n            RETURNING *\n        SQL);\n        $stmt->execute([\n            'player_id' => $player['id'], 'gq_id' => $gq['id'], 'option_id' => $optionId,\n            'is_correct' => $option['is_correct'] ? 't' : 'f',\n            'response_ms' => $clock['response_ms'], 'points' => $score['points'],\n            'streak_after' => $score['streak_after'],\n        ]);\n        $answer = $stmt->fetch();\n    } catch (PDOException $exception) {\n        if ($exception->getCode() === '23505') {\n            $dupStmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM answers WHERE game_player_id = :player_id AND game_question_id = :gq_id');\n            $dupStmt->execute(['player_id' => $player['id'], 'gq_id' => $gq['id']]);\n            return ['status' => 'duplicate', 'answer' => $dupStmt->fetch() ?: null];\n        }\n        throw $exception;\n    }\n\n    log_activity($pdo, 'answer_submitted', [\n        'actor_type' => 'player', 'actor_id' => (int) $player['id'], 'actor_label' => $player['nickname'],\n        'game_id' => (int) $game['id'], 'entity' => 'answers', 'entity_id' => (int) $answer['id'],\n        'details' => ['position' => (int) $gq['position'], 'correct' => (bool) $option['is_correct'],\n                      'response_ms' => (int) $clock['response_ms'], 'points' => $score['points']],\n    ]);\n    check_question_complete($pdo, (int) $game['id']);\n    return ['status' => 'ok', 'answer' => $answer];\n}\n\n/** Answer distribution for a game question: per option, count of picks. */\nfunction find_answer_distribution(PDO $pdo, int $gameQuestionId, array $options): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT option_id, count(*) AS picks\n        FROM answers WHERE game_question_id = :gq_id GROUP BY option_id\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['gq_id' => $gameQuestionId]);\n    $byOption = array_column($stmt->fetchAll(), 'picks', 'option_id');\n    $total = array_sum($byOption);\n    $rows = [];\n    foreach ($options as $option) {\n        $picks = (int) ($byOption[$option['id']] ?? 0);\n        $rows[] = $option + [\n            'picks' => $picks,\n            'pct'   => $total > 0 ? (int) round(100 * $picks / $total) : 0,\n        ];\n    }\n    return $rows;\n}\n\n/**\n * Live totals per player. Includes rank now and rank as of the previous position\n * (for leaderboard deltas). Live players only, ordered by current rank.\n */\nfunction find_scoreboard(PDO $pdo, int $gameId, int $currentPosition): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT gp.id, gp.nickname, gp.user_id,\n               COALESCE(sum(a.points_awarded), 0) AS total,\n               COALESCE(sum(a.points_awarded) FILTER (WHERE gq.position < :position), 0) AS prev_total,\n               COALESCE(max(a.streak_after) FILTER (WHERE gq.position = :position2), 0) AS current_streak\n        FROM game_players gp\n        LEFT JOIN answers a ON a.game_player_id = gp.id\n        LEFT JOIN game_questions gq ON gq.id = a.game_question_id\n        WHERE gp.game_id = :game_id AND gp.kicked_at IS NULL\n        GROUP BY gp.id\n        ORDER BY total DESC, gp.joined_at ASC\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['game_id' => $gameId, 'position' => $currentPosition, 'position2' => $currentPosition]);\n    $rows = $stmt->fetchAll();\n\n    $prevOrder = $rows;\n    usort($prevOrder, static fn (array $a, array $b): int => $b['prev_total'] <=> $a['prev_total']);\n    $prevRank = [];\n    foreach ($prevOrder as $index => $row) {\n        $prevRank[(int) $row['id']] = $index + 1;\n    }\n    foreach ($rows as $index => &$row) {\n        $row['rank']      = $index + 1;\n        $row['rank_delta'] = $prevRank[(int) $row['id']] - ($index + 1);   // + = moved up\n    }\n    return $rows;\n}\n\n/** Final podium (post-finalize): ranked live players. */\nfunction find_podium(PDO $pdo, int $gameId): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT id, nickname, user_id, final_score, final_rank\n        FROM game_players\n        WHERE game_id = :game_id AND kicked_at IS NULL AND final_rank IS NOT NULL\n        ORDER BY final_rank ASC, joined_at ASC\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['game_id' => $gameId]);\n    return $stmt->fetchAll();\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve the host console's current stage after running the lazy transition.\n * Returns ['game' => row, 'view' => template, 'data' => vars for it].\n */\nfunction find_host_stage(PDO $pdo, int $gameId): ?array\n{\n    check_question_complete($pdo, $gameId);\n    $game = find_game($pdo, $gameId);\n    if ($game === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n    $version = find_game_version($pdo, $gameId);\n    $base = ['game' => $game, 'version' => $version];\n\n    switch ($game['state']) {\n        case 'lobby':\n        case 'ended':\n        case 'aborted':\n            return $base + ['view' => 'games/partials/host-players.php',\n                            'data' => $base + ['players' => find_game_players($pdo, $gameId)]];\n        case 'question':\n            $gq = find_current_game_question($pdo, $game);\n            return $base + ['view' => 'games/partials/host-question.php',\n                            'data' => $base + [\n                                'gq'       => $gq,\n                                'answered' => count_answers_for($pdo, (int) $gq['id']),\n                                'live'     => count_live_players($pdo, $gameId),\n                            ]];\n        case 'reveal':\n            $gq = find_current_game_question($pdo, $game);\n            return $base + ['view' => 'games/partials/host-reveal.php',\n                            'data' => $base + [\n                                'gq'           => $gq,\n                                'distribution' => find_answer_distribution($pdo, (int) $gq['id'], $gq['options']),\n                                'live'         => count_live_players($pdo, $gameId),\n                            ]];\n        case 'leaderboard':\n            return $base + ['view' => 'games/partials/host-leaderboard.php',\n                            'data' => $base + [\n                                'scoreboard' => find_scoreboard($pdo, $gameId, (int) $game['current_position']),\n                                'isLast'     => (int) $game['current_position'] >= (int) $game['question_count'],\n                            ]];\n        case 'podium':\n            return $base + ['view' => 'games/partials/host-podium.php',\n                            'data' => $base + ['podium' => find_podium($pdo, $gameId)]];\n    }\n    return null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve one player's current stage (their poll + post-answer view).\n * Kicked/terminal handling stays in the controllers (S2). ['view','data','terminal'].\n */\nfunction find_player_stage(PDO $pdo, array $player, array $game): array\n{\n    check_question_complete($pdo, (int) $game['id']);\n    $game = find_game($pdo, (int) $game['id']);   // reload post-transition\n    $version = find_game_version($pdo, (int) $game['id']);\n    $base = ['game' => $game, 'player' => $player, 'version' => $version];\n\n    switch ($game['state']) {\n        case 'question':\n            $gq = find_current_game_question($pdo, $game);\n            $mine = find_player_answer($pdo, (int) $player['id'], (int) $gq['id']);\n            if ($mine !== null) {\n                return ['view' => 'play/locked-in.php', 'terminal' => false,\n                        'data' => $base + ['gq' => $gq, 'answer' => $mine]];\n            }\n            return ['view' => 'play/question.php', 'terminal' => false, 'data' => $base + ['gq' => $gq]];\n        case 'reveal':\n            $gq = find_current_game_question($pdo, $game);\n            return ['view' => 'play/reveal.php', 'terminal' => false,\n                    'data' => $base + [\n                        'gq'     => $gq,\n                        'answer' => find_player_answer($pdo, (int) $player['id'], (int) $gq['id']),\n                        'total'  => find_player_total($pdo, (int) $player['id']),\n                    ]];\n        case 'leaderboard':\n            $scoreboard = find_scoreboard($pdo, (int) $game['id'], (int) $game['current_position']);\n            $mine = null;\n            foreach ($scoreboard as $row) {\n                if ((int) $row['id'] === (int) $player['id']) {\n                    $mine = $row;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            return ['view' => 'play/leaderboard.php', 'terminal' => false, 'data' => $base + ['mine' => $mine]];\n        case 'podium':\n            $podium = find_podium($pdo, (int) $game['id']);\n            $mine = null;\n            foreach ($podium as $row) {\n                if ((int) $row['id'] === (int) $player['id']) {\n                    $mine = $row;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            return ['view' => 'play/podium.php', 'terminal' => true, 'data' => $base + ['mine' => $mine]];\n        default:\n            $players   = find_game_players($pdo, (int) $game['id']);\n            $liveCount = count(array_filter($players, static fn (array $p): bool => $p['kicked_at'] === null));\n            return ['view' => 'play/lobby.php', 'terminal' => false,\n                    'data' => $base + ['liveCount' => $liveCount]];\n    }\n}\n\nfunction find_player_answer(PDO $pdo, int $playerId, int $gameQuestionId): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT a.*, o.option_text, o.display_order\n        FROM answers a\n        JOIN question_options o ON o.id = a.option_id\n        WHERE a.game_player_id = :player_id AND a.game_question_id = :gq_id\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['player_id' => $playerId, 'gq_id' => $gameQuestionId]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    return $row === false ? null : $row;\n}\n\nfunction find_player_total(PDO $pdo, int $playerId): int\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT COALESCE(sum(points_awarded), 0) FROM answers WHERE game_player_id = :id');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $playerId]);\n    return (int) $stmt->fetchColumn();\n}\nEOF\nphp -l app/features/games/engine.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"\nphp -r '\nrequire \"app/features/games/engine.php\" and exit;' 2>/dev/null\n# Unit-test score_answer (pure '-' . $row['live_players'] . '-' . $row['answered'] . '-' . $row['remaining'];\n}'''\nassert old in src\nsrc = src.replace(old, new)\nopen('app/features/games/queries.php', 'w').write(src)\nprint(\"version function updated\")\nPY\nphp -l app/features/games/queries.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"\n\n# Host screen shell: host-lobby.php now wraps ANY stage (PIN card moves into the lobby stage).\ncat > app/views/games/partials/host-lobby.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\n/**\n * Host console SCREEN SHELL (S3 restructure): page-header + main-content wrapping\n * whatever stage is current. (int) $mine['final_rank'] : 0;\n$medals = [1 => 'warning', 2 => 'secondary', 3 => 'danger'];\n?>\n<div id=\"play-stage\">\n    <?php if ($mine !== null): ?>\n        <div class=\"avatar-text avatar-xl bg-<?= e($medals[$rank] ?? 'primary') ?> mx-auto mb-3\"><?= e($rank) ?></div>\n        <h2 class=\"fs-20 fw-bolder mb-2 text-center\" id=\"play-podium-title\">\n            <?= $rank === 1 ? 'Champion!' : 'You finished ' . e($rank) . e($suffix($rank)) ?>\n        </h2>\n        <p class=\"fs-14 text-center\" id=\"play-podium-score\"><?= e(number_format((int) $mine['final_score'])) ?> points</p>\n    <?php else: ?>\n        <h2 class=\"fs-20 fw-bolder mb-2 text-center\" id=\"play-podium-title\">Game over</h2>\n    <?php endif; ?>\n    <a href=\"/join\" id=\"play-podium-join-link\" class=\"btn btn-primary w-100 mt-3\">Join another game</a>\n</div>\nEOF\nfor f in app/views/play/question.php app/views/play/locked-in.php app/views/play/reveal.php app/views/play/leaderboard.php app/views/play/podium.php; do php -l $f | grep -v \"No syntax\"; done; echo \"player stages done\"",
  "description": "Write the five player stage views"
}

'');\n$player = $token !== '' ? find_game_player_by_token($pdo, $token) : null;\n\nif ($player === null || $player['kicked_at'] !== null) {\n    redirect('/join');\n}\n\n$game = find_game($pdo, (int) $player['game_id']);\nif ($game === null) {\n    redirect('/join');\n}\n\n$optionId = request_integer('option_id');\nif ($optionId !== null) {\n    insert_answer($pdo, $player, $optionId);   // late/duplicate/invalid all resolve to the true stage below\n}\n\n$stage = find_player_stage($pdo, $player, $game);\nif ($stage['terminal']) {\n    http_response_code(286);\n}\necho view($stage['view'], $stage['data']);\nEOF\n\npython3 - << 'PY'\nsrc = open('public/router.php').read()\nold = \"    '/play/state'            => '/play/state.php',\"\nassert old in src or \"'/play/state'\" in src\nif old in src:\n    src = src.replace(old, old + \"\\n    '/play/answer'           => '/play/answer.php',\")\nelse:\n    # alignment may differ; insert after the /play/state line generically\n    lines = src.split('\\n')\n    for i, l in enumerate(lines):\n        if \"'/play/state'\" in l:\n            indent = l[:len(l) - len(l.lstrip())]\n            lines.insert(i + 1, indent + \"'/play/answer' => '/play/answer.php',\")\n            break\n    src = '\\n'.join(lines)\nopen('public/router.php', 'w').write(src)\nprint(\"router: /play/answer added\")\nPY\nfor f in public/games/host.php public/games/host-state.php public/games/advance.php public/play/index.php public/play/state.php public/play/answer.php public/router.php; do php -l $f | grep -v \"No syntax\"; done; echo \"endpoints done\"",

'/app/features/games/engine.php';\")\nimport re\n# Replace the trailing host-players render (with whatever vars) by the stage resolver.\npattern = re.compile(r\"echo view\\('games/partials/host-players\\.php', \\[[^;]*?\\]\\);\", re.S)\nsrc2 = pattern.sub(\"\"\"\\$stage = find_host_stage(\\$pdo, \\$id);\necho view(\\$stage['view'], \\$stage['data']);\"\"\", src, count=1)\nassert src2 != src, \"no replacement made in $f.php\"\nopen('public/games/$f.php', 'w').write(src2)\nprint(\"$f.php fixed\")\nPY\ndone\nphp -l public/games/kick.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"; php -l public/games/abort.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"\ngrep -n '\\$id' public/games/kick.php | head -3; grep -n '\\$id' public/games/abort.php | head -3",

## 🤖 Claude

**The game is playable. Slice S3 — the live game loop — is built, verified with a complete game, and pushed** (`298492c`). Your group could run a real CCAO-F game night tonight: create a game, project the host console, everyone joins at `/join` on their phones, and the full Kahoot arc runs — countdown questions, speed scoring, streaks, reveals with the explanation as the teaching moment, leaderboards, podium.

I verified it by actually playing a 5-question game across two browser tabs:

- **The host console became a projector screen**: question 1/5 with the stem huge in the center, four numbered color options, a draining countdown bar, and a live "0 of 1 answered" counter ticking every second.
- **Speed scoring is real and server-clocked**: a ~7.9-second answer earned 604 points, a ~1.1-second answer earned 944 — both exactly matching the Kahoot formula, computed from server timestamps so nobody's phone clock matters.
- **Both reveal triggers fired**: questions I let expire flipped to reveal on the deadline (the lazy, row-locked transition — no cron, no WebSockets, just the 1-second poll), and questions I answered flipped **instantly** because every live player was in. The activity log recorded which trigger fired for every question.
- **Streak semantics survived the tricky case**: a missed question leaves no answer row, yet correctly breaks the streak (verified live — the next correct answer restarted at streak 1, no bonus). The pure scoring function passes 8 unit cases including the sub-500ms full-points rule and the +100 streak bonus.
- **The full arc completed**: reveal (distribution bars, correct answer badged, the explanation card), leaderboard with rank-delta arrows, the podium with a gold-medal champion at 1,548 points, Finish → game ended with final scores and ranks written to the database.
- **The 375px player screen is the real thing**: four giant full-width color buttons, question counter, countdown — no horizontal scroll, thumb-sized targets.

One fix worth mentioning: converting the host console to stage-based rendering broke three S2 endpoints that still rendered the old lobby layout directly — all now route through the stage resolver, so every host response (save, kick, abort, advance, poll) always returns the true current stage.

What's left in Phase 3 is the supporting cast: **S4 game history & reports** (the podium and per-question breakdowns after the fact), **S5 readiness analytics** (the "is the group ready to book CCAO-F" dashboards), and **S6 scenario rounds**. S4 and S5 are worker-class slices per the plan. Say "continue" and I'll run S4 — or if you'd rather try a game night with the group first, everything you need is already running.

- `/Users/user/study-game/app/views/games/report.php` — screen shell (page-header + main-content), header card section inline
- `/Users/user/study-game/app/views/games/partials/report-ranking.php` — podium + full ranking table
- `/Users/user/study-game/app/views/games/partials/report-questions.php` — per-question breakdown with distribution mini-bars

**Files modified:**
- `/Users/user/study-game/public/router.php` — added `#^/games/(\d+)$#` → `games/view.php`, placed with the other games regex routes
- `/Users/user/study-game/app/views/games/partials/row.php` — ended/aborted rows now link to `/games/{id}` (same hx-get/hx-push-url pattern as live rows→host) instead of the S4 tooltip placeholder; nothing else in the file touched

- CLI check matched every value the spec specified: game 45 (`ended`) → ranking[0] nickname `Quizmaster`, total `1548`, rank `1`; 5 question rows; position 2 `correct_pct=100`, `avg_response_s=7.9`; position 1 `correct_pct=0`, `avg_response_s=null`.
- Game 44 (aborted, sole player kicked) → `find_game_report` returns an empty (but error-free) computed ranking; games 46/47 also render without error.
- Rendered all four view templates directly with `E_ALL`/`display_errors=1` — no notices/warnings, all expected `game-report-*` ids present, values correct.

**Open Questions appended to `docs/build-specs/game-history.md`** (4 items, all low-risk substitutions I made and flagged rather than blocking on):
1. `find_game_question_stats`'s literal DATA SHAPES key list omits a domain field, but the layout requires a domain column — added `domain_name` (joined via questions→domains).
2. `find_game_report`'s ranking-row key list omits a member-name field, but the layout requires "nickname (+ member name if claimed)" — added `member_name` (LEFT JOIN users, null when unclaimed).

4. The ranking table's "correct/total" column isn't precisely defined — implemented as `correct_count`/`game.question_count` (not answers received), consistent with amendment 5's "unanswered counts against you" principle.

Nothing else was noticed as needing action — schema, engine.php, queries.php, and the questions/ directory were left untouched, and the dev server was neither restarted nor stopped.

<details><summary>🔧 tool result</summary>

```
push-url true: 0 | modal: 0 | confirm-in-analytics: 0
POST endpoints in analytics (must be 0): 0
analytics.md
game-history.md
game-loop.md
games-lobby.md
questions.md
scenarios.md
-- member_readiness(PDO, int userId, int examId)
-- CLAIMED answers only (game_players.user_id = :user_id) per amendment 3. One row per
-- domain of the exam. active_question_count/seen_question_count scope to status='active'
-- questions only, matching the member grid's "seen/total active" column.
SELECT
    d.id                                                                             AS domain_id,
    d.name                                                                           AS domain_name,
    d.weight_pct,
    d.display_order,
    count(DISTINCT q.id) FILTER (WHERE q.status = 'active')                          AS active_question_count,
    count(DISTINCT CASE WHEN q.status = 'active' AND a.id IS NOT NULL THEN q.id END) AS seen_question_count,
    count(a.id)                                                                      AS answered_count,
    count(a.id) FILTER (WHERE a.is_correct)                                          AS correct_count
FROM domains d
LEFT JOIN questions q       ON q.domain_id = d.id
LEFT JOIN game_questions gq ON gq.question_id = q.id
LEFT JOIN answers a         ON a.game_question_id = gq.id
                            AND EXISTS (
                                SELECT 1 FROM game_players gp
                                WHERE gp.id = a.game_player_id AND gp.user_id = :user_id
                            )
WHERE d.exam_id = :exam_id
GROUP BY d.id
ORDER BY d.display_order
```
</details>

The user (part of a Claude Partner Network study group) is building a Kahoot-style live game-show web app so the group can study together for the Anthropic Claude certification exams (CCAO-F, CCDV-F, CCAR-F, CCAR-P — Pearson VUE, 120 min, passing 720/1000). Planning started 2026-08-23 in /Users/user/study-game.

**Why:** The game doubles as group study analytics — question bank tagged by exam + domain weight, per-player answer history reveals exam readiness.

**Scope decision (2026-08-23):** First exam to seed and play: CCAO-F (Claude Certified Associate, Foundations) — domains: Output Evaluation & Validation 21%, Workflow Integration & Solution Design 16%, Governance/Risk/Responsible Use 15%, Prompting & Task Execution 14%, Product & Model Selection 12%, Configuration & Knowledge Management 12%, Troubleshooting & Optimization 10%. The group is targeting all four exams. Each game/session selects exactly one exam to study for; the question bank spans all four, tagged by exam + domain, and rounds draw questions weighted to the selected exam's official domain blueprint.

**Progress (2026-08-23):** Phases 0–2 approved and built (schema, auth incl. Google OIDC + TOTP, app shell). Phase 3 S1 (question bank exemplar) built and verified: 69 active CCAO-F questions seeded per blueprint, correct answers shuffled. Repo: github.com/maludb-ed/study-game (private). Dev login: edward.honour@gmail.com / cert-arena-dev-2026 (local only). S2 (game creation, weighted draw, PIN join, live 1s-poll lobby) worker-built per spec, conformance-reviewed, verified live in two tabs, pushed. S3 (live game loop: state machine, speed scoring, streaks, reveal/leaderboard/podium) planning-class built and verified with a full 5-question game in two tabs; pushed. The app now runs real game nights end to end. Next: S4 game history & reports (worker), then S5 analytics, S6 scenarios, Phase 4 MCP + assistant.

**How to apply:** Build on the standard plugin stack (PostgreSQL 17 + MaluDB, Apache/PHP/Bootstrap 5.3/HTMX, MCP servers + AMA agent). Realtime via short-interval HTMX polling first; SSE only if polling proves insufficient. Host-paced game state machine: lobby → question → reveal → leaderboard → podium. Kahoot scoring: floor((1 − (t/T)/2) × 1000).

## 🤖 Claude

**S5 — readiness analytics — is built, verified, and pushed** (`2c27e23`), following S4 earlier in this turn. Phase 3 now has five of its six slices done, all conformance-gated.

What the two worker-built slices delivered:

**S4 — Game reports.** Every finished or aborted game now has a permanent report at `/games/{id}`: header card (exam, date, host, settings, state), the podium and full ranking (finalized scores for ended games, live-computed for aborted ones), and a per-question breakdown — correct rate counted against all players so unanswered questions hurt the number honestly, average response time, and distribution mini-bars with each stem linking back to its question in the bank. Ended rows in the games list now link straight to their reports.

**S5 — Readiness analytics.** Three screens, and this is the part that makes game night more than fun:

- **Group Readiness** (`/analytics/`) — exam tabs, stat tiles (games played, questions answered, group weighted score, members over 720), and a domain performance table with 30-day trend arrows and a weakest-member hint per domain. It's honest about its data: group numbers count every answer, member hints count claimed answers only, and the footnote says so.
- **Member readiness** (`/analytics/members/{id}`) — the banner implements the locked formula: weighted accuracy across the exam's blueprint (unseen domains count as zero), scaled to the exam's 100–1,000 scale against the real 720 passing bar, with a 60% blueprint-coverage gate before it will claim anything. Right now yours correctly says "Not enough data yet — 0% coverage," because the test game's player never claimed an identity.
- **Drill list** (`/analytics/drill`) — most-missed questions (minimum 3 plays), which will become the group's pre-exam review sheet once real game nights accumulate data.

Both workers behaved exactly as the process intends — the S5 worker even caught a factual error in my own verification instructions (I said "4 answered," the data says 2), resolved it from the authoritative restatement, and documented the call. The analytics SQL lives in standalone files under `app/features/analytics/sql/`, which is deliberate: the Phase 4 records-MCP server will load those same files from Python, so the app screens and the MCP tools can never disagree about what "readiness" means.

**One slice left in Phase 3: S6, scenario rounds** — the CCAR-F-style format where a scenario card precedes a linked block of questions, which matters once you move past the Associate exam. After that, Phase 4 wires up the two read-MCP servers, the actions server, and the AMA assistant. Say "continue" for S6.

# Build spec: scenarios slice (S6)

Exemplar to replicate: questions (S1) for CRUD; game-loop (S3) for the in-game part.
Schema tables: scenarios, questions (scenario_id), game_questions.

## Screens

| Screen id | Canonical URL | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| scenario-list | /scenarios/ | List: title | exam | status | linked questions | updated |
| scenario-add | /scenarios/new | Form |
| scenario-edit | /scenarios/{id}/edit | Same form pre-filled |
| scenario-view | /scenarios/{id} | Body + its questions in order |

## Form
- exam_id | select | required | scenario-form-field-exam
- title | text | required 5–120 chars | scenario-form-field-title
- body | textarea 8 rows | required 50–4000 chars | scenario-form-field-body
- status | select draft/active/retired | scenario-form-field-status
- Questions are attached FROM the question form (S1's form gains a scenario select filtered by exam — the one sanctioned S1 touch, listed here so the worker changes exactly that)

## Game integration (extends S3)
- The draw keeps scenario questions together: when a drawn question has scenario_id, pull its sibling active questions (same scenario) into consecutive positions, capped at the domain target
- New state-machine wrinkle: entering a question whose scenario differs from the previous question's inserts a scenario intro phase — host shows title+body full screen, players see "Read the host screen"; host Next proceeds to the question. Implement as a nullable games.scenario_intro_for (bigint) column — THE ONE SCHEMA CHANGE, pre-approved here — set when entering, cleared on Next
- Reveal and scoring unchanged

## Files (exactly these)
- public/scenarios/index.php · form.php · save.php · status.php · delete.php
- app/features/scenarios/queries.php
- app/views/scenarios/page.php · partials/table.php · row.php · form.php · saved.php
- app/views/games/partials/host-scenario.php · app/views/play/scenario.php
- db/007_scenario_intro.sql (the pre-approved column)

## Query functions (signatures fixed)
- find_scenarios(PDO, exam_id=0, status='', page=1): array
- find_scenario(PDO, int id): ?array
- insert_scenario(PDO, int exam_id, string title, string body, string status, int created_by): array
- update_scenario(PDO, int id, …): array
- set_scenario_status(PDO, int id, string status): array
- delete_scenario(PDO, int id): bool   — refuses when questions reference it

## Action manifest entries
- Screens above; actions scenario_create/scenario_update/scenario_retire (undo per question pattern), scenario_delete (confirm)

## Activity log events
- scenario_created/updated/status_changed/deleted, scenario_shown (in game)

## Status vocabulary mapping
- draft → secondary · active → success · retired → dark

## Out of scope
- Scenario-only games. Timed reading. Images.

## Open Questions (must be EMPTY before a worker starts)
- (none)

## 🤖 Claude

Nav placement is clear. Building S6 — spec amendments and the scenarios CRUD layer first:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\npython3 - << 'PY'\nsrc = open('app/views/layout.php').read()\nold = \"                            <?= $navItem('nav-question-import', '/questions/import', 'Import') ?>\\n\"\nnew = old + \"                            <?= $navItem('nav-scenario-list', '/scenarios/', 'Scenarios') ?>\\n\"\nassert old in src\nopen('app/views/layout.php', 'w').write(src.replace(old, new))\nprint(\"nav item added\")\nPY\n\ncat >> docs/build-specs/scenarios.md << 'EOF'\n\n## Exemplar-alignment amendments (planning-class — this slice was BUILT planning-class because it cuts into the S3 engine)\n\n1. **Status vocabulary corrected** to the locked design-decisions mapping: draft → dark · active → success · retired → secondary (the table above had draft/retired swapped).\n2. **Intro-phase mechanics (locked):** entering position N whose question's scenario_id is non-null and differs from position N−1's scenario_id sets `games.scenario_intro_for = scenario_id`, `state='question'`, and leaves `question_started_at`/`question_deadline` NULL (game_questions unstamped) — the clock does NOT run during reading. `check_question_complete` returns early while `question_deadline IS NULL`. `insert_answer` naturally rejects (timing check fails on NULL deadline). The host intro stage's Next posts `expected_state='scenario_intro'`; `advance_game` handles it when `state='question' AND scenario_intro_for IS NOT NULL`: clears the column, stamps both deadline pairs, logs `question_shown`. `find_game_version` coalesces the remaining-seconds component to 0 while unstamped. Stage resolution: `state='question' AND scenario_intro_for` → host-scenario.php / play/scenario.php.\n3. **Draw sibling rule (locked):** per domain, after the normal target take — if any taken question has a scenario_id, its active same-domain siblings REPLACE lowest-priority non-scenario picks in that domain, never exceeding the domain target; the final shuffle shuffles GROUPS (scenario groups + singleton questions), keeping each group's questions consecutive in scenario-defined order (question id ascending).\n4. **S1 form touch mechanics:** the question form's exam-dependent selects (domain + scenario) are wrapped in `div#question-form-field-dependent`; the exam select's fragment call moves from `/questions/domain-options` to `/questions/dependent-fields` (new file `public/questions/dependent-fields.php` REPLACES `domain-options.php` — file deleted, route replaced). The scenario select (`question-form-field-scenario`, optional, \"No scenario\") lists draft+active scenarios of the chosen exam.\n5. **Scenario view shows** body + linked questions ordered by question id, each linking to `/questions/{id}`; question counts on the list are total (any status).\n6. **delete_scenario** refuses (returns false) when any question references it — UI only offers Delete for drafts with zero linked questions, mirroring S1.\nEOF\necho \"spec amended\"\n\nmkdir -p app/features/scenarios app/views/scenarios/partials public/scenarios\ncat > app/features/scenarios/queries.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nconst SCENARIOS_PAGE_SIZE = 25;\n\n/** List with filters + pagination. Returns ['rows' => [...], 'total' => int]. */\nfunction find_scenarios(PDO $pdo, int $examId = 0, string $status = '', int $page = 1): array\n{\n    $where  = [];\n    $params = [];\n    if ($examId > 0) { $where[] = 's.exam_id = :exam_id'; $params['exam_id'] = $examId; }\n    if ($status !== '' && in_array($status, ['draft', 'active', 'retired'], true)) {\n        $where[] = 's.status = :status';\n        $params['status'] = $status;\n    }\n    $whereSql = $where === [] ? '' : 'WHERE ' . implode(' AND ', $where);\n\n    $countStmt = $pdo->prepare(\"SELECT count(*) FROM scenarios s {$whereSql}\");\n    $countStmt->execute($params);\n    $total = (int) $countStmt->fetchColumn();\n\n    $page   = max(1, $page);\n    $offset = ($page - 1) * SCENARIOS_PAGE_SIZE;\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<SQL\n        SELECT s.id, s.title, s.status, s.updated_at, e.code AS exam_code,\n               (SELECT count(*) FROM questions q WHERE q.scenario_id = s.id) AS question_count\n        FROM scenarios s\n        JOIN exams e ON e.id = s.exam_id\n        {$whereSql}\n        ORDER BY s.updated_at DESC\n        LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset\n    SQL);\n    foreach ($params as $key => $value) {\n        $stmt->bindValue($key, $value);\n    }\n    $stmt->bindValue('limit', SCENARIOS_PAGE_SIZE, PDO::PARAM_INT);\n    $stmt->bindValue('offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);\n    $stmt->execute();\n\n    return ['rows' => $stmt->fetchAll(), 'total' => $total];\n}\n\n/** One scenario with its linked questions (id order = play order). */\nfunction find_scenario(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT s.*, e.code AS exam_code, e.name AS exam_name, u.display_name AS author_name\n        FROM scenarios s\n        JOIN exams e ON e.id = s.exam_id\n        LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = s.created_by\n        WHERE s.id = :id\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $scenario = $stmt->fetch();\n    if ($scenario === false) {\n        return null;\n    }\n    $qStmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT q.id, q.stem, q.status, q.difficulty, d.name AS domain_name\n        FROM questions q\n        JOIN domains d ON d.id = q.domain_id\n        WHERE q.scenario_id = :id\n        ORDER BY q.id\n    SQL);\n    $qStmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    $scenario['questions'] = $qStmt->fetchAll();\n    return $scenario;\n}\n\nfunction insert_scenario(PDO $pdo, int $examId, string $title, string $body, string $status, int $createdBy): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        INSERT INTO scenarios (exam_id, title, body, status, created_by)\n        VALUES (:exam_id, :title, :body, :status, :created_by)\n        RETURNING id, exam_id, title, body, status\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute([\n        'exam_id' => $examId, 'title' => $title, 'body' => $body,\n        'status' => $status, 'created_by' => $createdBy,\n    ]);\n    return $stmt->fetch();\n}\n\n/** Returns ['before' => row, 'after' => row]. */\nfunction update_scenario(PDO $pdo, int $id, int $examId, string $title, string $body, string $status): array\n{\n    $before = find_scenario($pdo, $id);\n    if ($before === null) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('Scenario not found.');\n    }\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        UPDATE scenarios\n        SET exam_id = :exam_id, title = :title, body = :body, status = :status\n        WHERE id = :id\n        RETURNING id, exam_id, title, body, status\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id, 'exam_id' => $examId, 'title' => $title, 'body' => $body, 'status' => $status]);\n    return ['before' => $before, 'after' => $stmt->fetch()];\n}\n\nfunction set_scenario_status(PDO $pdo, int $id, string $status): array\n{\n    if (!in_array($status, ['draft', 'active', 'retired'], true)) {\n        throw new InvalidArgumentException('Bad status.');\n    }\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('UPDATE scenarios SET status = :status WHERE id = :id RETURNING id, status');\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id, 'status' => $status]);\n    $row = $stmt->fetch();\n    if ($row === false) {\n        throw new RuntimeException('Scenario not found.');\n    }\n    return $row;\n}\n\n/** Drafts only, and only when no question references the scenario. */\nfunction delete_scenario(PDO $pdo, int $id): bool\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        DELETE FROM scenarios s\n        WHERE s.id = :id AND s.status = 'draft'\n          AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM questions q WHERE q.scenario_id = s.id)\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);\n    return $stmt->rowCount() === 1;\n}\n\n/** Draft+active scenarios of one exam — the question form's attach options. */\nfunction find_scenarios_for_exam(PDO $pdo, int $examId): array\n{\n    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(<<<'SQL'\n        SELECT id, title, status FROM scenarios\n        WHERE exam_id = :exam_id AND status IN ('draft', 'active')\n        ORDER BY title\n    SQL);\n    $stmt->execute(['exam_id' => $examId]);\n    return $stmt->fetchAll();\n}\nEOF\nphp -l app/features/scenarios/queries.php | grep -v \"No syntax\"; echo \"scenarios queries done\"",
  "description": "Add nav item, amend spec, apply migration, write scenarios query layer"
}

'/app/features/scenarios/queries.php';\n\n$user = require_login();\n$pdo  = db();\n\n$id = request_integer('id');\n$scenario = $id === null ? null : find_scenario($pdo, $id);\nif ($scenario === null) {\n    http_response_code(404);\n    exit('Scenario not found.');\n}\n\nlog_screen_view($pdo, 'scenario-view');\n$content = view('scenarios/partials/view.php', ['scenario' => $scenario, 'saved' => null]);\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    echo $content;\n    exit;\n}\necho view('layout.php', ['title' => 'Scenario #' . $id, 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-scenario-list', 'user' => $user]);\nEOF\n\ncat > public/scenarios/form.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . 'Add Scenario' : 'Edit Scenario', 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-scenario-list', 'user' => $user]);\nEOF\n\ncat > public/scenarios/save.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/scenarios/queries.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/exams/queries.php';\n\n$user = require_admin();\nrequire_post();\nverify_csrf();\n$pdo = db();\n\n$id     = request_integer('id');\n$examId = request_integer('exam_id') ?? 0;\n$title  = trim((string) ($_POST['title'] ?? ''));\n$body   = trim((string) ($_POST['body'] ?? ''));\n$status = (string) ($_POST['status'] ?? 'draft');\n\n$errors = [];\nif ($examId <= 0)                                        { $errors[] = 'Choose an exam.'; }\nif (mb_strlen($title) < 5 || mb_strlen($title) > 120)    { $errors[] = 'The title must be 5–120 characters.'; }\nif (mb_strlen($body) < 50 || mb_strlen($body) > 4000)    { $errors[] = 'The scenario must be 50–4000 characters.'; }\nif (!in_array($status, ['draft', 'active', 'retired'], true)) { $errors[] = 'Bad status.'; }\n\nif ($errors !== []) {\n    $content = view('scenarios/partials/form.php', [\n        'scenario' => ['id' => $id, 'exam_id' => $examId, 'title' => $title, 'body' => $body, 'status' => $status],\n        'exams'    => find_exams_with_counts($pdo),\n        'errors'   => $errors,\n    ]);\n    if (is_htmx_request()) {\n        header('Vary: HX-Request');\n        echo $content;\n        exit;\n    }\n    echo view('layout.php', ['title' => 'Scenario', 'content' => $content, 'active' => 'nav-scenario-list', 'user' => $user]);\n    exit;\n}\n\ntry {\n    $pdo->beginTransaction();\n    if ($id === null) {\n        $scenario = insert_scenario($pdo, $examId, $title, $body, $status, (int) $user['id']);\n        $savedId  = (int) $scenario['id'];\n        log_activity($pdo, 'scenario_created', [\n            'screen' => 'scenario-add', 'entity' => 'scenarios', 'entity_id' => $savedId, 'after' => $scenario,\n        ]);\n        $savedAction = 'created';\n    } else {\n        $change  = update_scenario($pdo, $id, $examId, $title, $body, $status);\n        $savedId = $id;\n        log_activity($pdo, 'scenario_updated', [\n            'screen' => 'scenario-edit', 'entity' => 'scenarios', 'entity_id' => $id,\n            'before' => array_diff_key($change['before'], ['questions' => 1]), 'after' => $change['after'],\n        ]);\n        $savedAction = 'updated';\n    }\n    $pdo->commit();\n} catch (Throwable $exception) {\n    if ($pdo->inTransaction()) { $pdo->rollBack(); }\n    error_log('scenario save: ' . $exception->getMessage());\n    http_response_code(500);\n    exit('The scenario could not be saved.');\n}\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    header('HX-Push-Url: /scenarios/' . $savedId);\n    echo view('scenarios/partials/view.php', ['scenario' => find_scenario($pdo, $savedId), 'saved' => $savedAction]);\n    exit;\n}\nheader('Location: /scenarios/' . $savedId);\nexit;\nEOF\n\ncat > public/scenarios/status.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/scenarios/queries.php';\n\n$user = require_admin();\nrequire_post();\nverify_csrf();\n$pdo = db();\n\n$id     = request_integer('id');\n$status = (string) ($_POST['status'] ?? '');\nif ($id === null || !in_array($status, ['draft', 'active', 'retired'], true)) {\n    http_response_code(400);\n    exit('Bad request.');\n}\n$before = find_scenario($pdo, $id);\nif ($before === null) {\n    http_response_code(404);\n    exit('Scenario not found.');\n}\n\ntry {\n    $pdo->beginTransaction();\n    set_scenario_status($pdo, $id, $status);\n    log_activity($pdo, 'scenario_status_changed', [\n        'screen' => 'scenario-view', 'entity' => 'scenarios', 'entity_id' => $id,\n        'before' => ['status' => $before['status']], 'after' => ['status' => $status],\n    ]);\n    $pdo->commit();\n} catch (Throwable $exception) {\n    if ($pdo->inTransaction()) { $pdo->rollBack(); }\n    throw $exception;\n}\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('Vary: HX-Request');\n    header('HX-Push-Url: /scenarios/' . $id);\n    echo view('scenarios/partials/view.php', ['scenario' => find_scenario($pdo, $id), 'saved' => null]);\n    exit;\n}\nheader('Location: /scenarios/' . $id);\nexit;\nEOF\n\ncat > public/scenarios/delete.php << 'EOF'\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/bootstrap.php';\nrequire_once dirname(__DIR__, 2) . '/app/features/scenarios/queries.php';\n\n$user = require_admin();\nrequire_post();\nverify_csrf();\n$pdo = db();\n\n$id = request_integer('id');\nif ($id === null) {\n    http_response_code(400);\n    exit('Bad request.');\n}\n$before = find_scenario($pdo, $id);\nif ($before === null) {\n    http_response_code(404);\n    exit('Scenario not found.');\n}\n\ntry {\n    $pdo->beginTransaction();\n    $deleted = delete_scenario($pdo, $id);\n    if ($deleted) {\n        log_activity($pdo, 'scenario_deleted', [\n            'screen' => 'scenario-view', 'entity' => 'scenarios', 'entity_id' => $id,\n            'before' => array_diff_key($before, ['questions' => 1]),\n        ]);\n    }\n    $pdo->commit();\n} catch (Throwable $exception) {\n    if ($pdo->inTransaction()) { $pdo->rollBack(); }\n    throw $exception;\n}\n\nif (!$deleted) {\n    if (is_htmx_request()) {\n        header('Vary: HX-Request');\n        echo view('scenarios/partials/view.php', ['scenario' => find_scenario($pdo, $id), 'saved' => null]);\n        exit;\n    }\n    header('Location: /scenarios/' . $id);\n    exit;\n}\n\nif (is_htmx_request()) {\n    header('HX-Location: {\"path\": \"/scenarios/\", \"target\": \"#page-content\"}');\n    exit;\n}\nheader('Location: /scenarios/');\nexit;\nEOF\nfor f in public/scenarios/*.php; do php -l $f | grep -v \"No syntax\"; done; echo \"scenario endpoints done\"",

| game_kick_player | POST `/games/{id}/kick.php` | player nickname | none (rejoin is possible) | **yes** |

| navigate | (registry above) | screen, params | n/a | no |
| undo_last | (inverse from manifest + activity log) | — | n/a | no |

## 🤖 Claude

The `$$` got interpolated by PHP's double-quoted strings — and my quick probe committed a junk row. Cleaning that up and rewriting the test as a file:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

## 🤖 Claude

`insert_game` owns its transaction (PIN-retry design), so the test can't wrap it. Running unwrapped with tagged cleanup instead:

```json

## 🤖 Claude

Question 77 linked. Adding the second via SQL (UI path proven), then rigging a game to test the intro live:

<details><summary>🔧 Bash</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/user/study-game\npsql -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d studygame << 'SQL'\nWITH q AS (\n    INSERT INTO questions (exam_id, domain_id, scenario_id, stem, explanation, difficulty, status, source)\n    SELECT 1, d.id, 4,\n        'After adding review, Acme wants to measure whether draft quality is improving. What should they track?',\n        'Improvement is measured against a baseline: sampled review pass-rate and edit distance over time show whether drafts need less correction, tied to the pre-rollout numbers.',\n        'medium', 'active', 'claude-generated 2026-08'\n    FROM domains d WHERE d.exam_id = 1 AND d.name = 'Output Evaluation and Validation'\n    RETURNING id\n)\nINSERT INTO question_options (question_id, option_text, is_correct, display_order)\nSELECT q.id, v.t, v.c, v.o FROM q, (VALUES\n    ('The number of emails sent per day', false, 1),\n    ('Review pass-rate and correction effort over time, against the pre-rollout baseline', true, 2),\n    ('How many agents like the tool', false, 3),\n    ('Total tokens consumed', false, 4)\n) AS v(t, c, o);\nSQL\n# Create a 5-question game via the engine, then rig positions 2-3 to the scenario pair.\nphp -r '\nrequire \"app/config.php\"; require \"app/db.php\"; require \"app/http.php\"; require \"app/auth.php\"; require \"app/log.php\";\nrequire \"app/features/games/engine.php\";\n$pdo = db();\n$g = insert_game($pdo, 1, 1, 5, 60, true);\n$pair = $pdo->query(\"SELECT id FROM questions WHERE scenario_id = 4 ORDER BY id\")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);\n// Rig: keep whatever is at position 1, put the scenario pair at 2 and 3.\n$pdo->beginTransaction();\n$pdo->exec(\"DELETE FROM game_questions WHERE game_id = {$g[\"id\"]} AND question_id IN (\" . implode(\",\", $pair) . \")\");\n$rows = $pdo->query(\"SELECT id FROM game_questions WHERE game_id = {$g[\"id\"]} ORDER BY position\")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);\n// Reassign: first remaining row -> pos 1, pair -> 2,3, remaining rows -> 4,5 (via temp offset to dodge unique).\n$pdo->exec(\"UPDATE game_questions SET position = position + 100 WHERE game_id = {$g[\"id\"]}\");\n$pos = 0; $updates = [];\n$keep = array_slice($rows, 0, 3);\n$stmt = $pdo->prepare(\"UPDATE game_questions SET position = :p WHERE id = :id\");\n$stmt->execute([\"p\" => 1, \"id\" => $keep[0]]);\n$ins = $pdo->prepare(\"INSERT INTO game_questions (game_id, question_id, position) VALUES (:g, :q, :p)\");\n$ins->execute([\"g\" => $g[\"id\"], \"q\" => $pair[0], \"p\" => 2]);\n$ins->execute([\"g\" => $g[\"id\"], \"q\" => $pair[1], \"p\" => 3]);\n$stmt->execute([\"p\" => 4, \"id\" => $keep[1]]);\n$stmt->execute([\"p\" => 5, \"id\" => $keep[2]]);\n$pdo->exec(\"DELETE FROM game_questions WHERE game_id = {$g[\"id\"]} AND position > 100\");\n$pdo->commit();\necho $g[\"id\"] . \"|\" . $g[\"pin\"] . PHP_EOL;\n' 2>/dev/null\npsql -Atd studygame -c \"select gq.position, coalesce(q.scenario_id::text,'-') from game_questions gq join questions q on q.id=gq.question_id where gq.game_id=(select max(id) from games) order by gq.position;\"",
  "description": "Add second scenario question; create and rig a test game"
}
```

## 🤖 Claude

**S6 — scenario rounds — is built, verified, and pushed** (`bfa9d13`). **That completes Phase 3: all six slices are done, and the app is feature-complete for game nights.**

I built this one myself rather than delegating, because it cuts into the S3 game engine. What shipped:

- **Scenario CRUD** — a Scenarios section in the Question Bank nav: list with filters, add/edit form (title + the reading passage + status), and a detail view showing the linked questions in play order. Questions attach from the question form, whose exam-dependent selects (domain + scenario) now swap together as one fragment when you change the exam.
- **The draw keeps scenario blocks together.** When a scenario question is drawn, its active same-domain siblings replace the domain's lowest-priority picks — capped at the domain's blueprint target — and the final shuffle shuffles *groups*, so a scenario's questions always land in consecutive positions. I property-tested this: 40 draws, 15 scenario appearances, zero adjacency violations, every block correctly capped at 2.
- **The intro phase, with a stopped clock.** Entering a question that opens a new scenario shows a full-screen reading stage on the projector — title, passage, "the timer starts when the host continues" — while players see "Scenario time — read the host screen." No countdown runs and no answers are accepted until the host clicks **Start the Question**. The next question of the *same* scenario skips the intro and goes straight to the buttons, which I verified live: intro at position 2, no intro at position 3, and the activity log shows exactly one `scenario_shown`.

The first real scenario ("Acme support rollout," two Output-Evaluation questions) is live in the CCAO-F bank, so your next game night has a chance of demoing the format naturally. Conformance is clean across the whole phase: zero `hx-push-url="true"`, zero modals, confirms only on destructive actions, CSRF everywhere it belongs, 375px verified.

**What's left is Phase 4**, the memory-and-assistant layer: the two read-only MCP servers (record memory over PostgreSQL — loading the same SQL files the analytics screens use — and activity memory over MaluDB), the localhost actions server from the manifest, the unified Claude Agent SDK assistant behind the command bar on every screen, and the settings screen that publishes MCP endpoints + tokens so the study group can point their own Claude at the group's readiness data. That's the phase that makes this a proper SaaS Plus+ app rather than just a quiz site. Say "continue" and I'll start it.
